          _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
            (sideComplement_finrank I copies U A).symm))
      (fun K => extension_nonempty K ht (by
        calc
          2 * h ≤ 2 * J := by omega
          _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
            (sideComplement_finrank I copies U A).symm))
      (transportedCenterTable I copies U A C)
      (transportedLeafTable I copies U A T)
  have haverage :
      ActualTaggedMZSideDraw.sideConditionalDensity I copies U t h k C T ≤
        ∑ A : SideComplement I copies U,
          ((1 : Rat) / Fintype.card (SideComplement I copies U)) * D A := by
    have hAvgBound := sideConditionalDensity_le_uniformComplement_average_StarDensity
      (k := k) I copies U ht hh C T
    change ActualTaggedMZSideDraw.sideConditionalDensity I copies U t h k C T ≤
      ∑ A : SideComplement I copies U,
        (1 / Fintype.card (SideComplement I copies U) : Rat) *
          StarDensity (V := A.1) (k := k)
            (grass_nonempty_of_le (by
              calc
                t ≤ 2 * h := ht
                _ ≤ 2 * J := by omega
                _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
                  (sideComplement_finrank I copies U A).symm))
            (fun K => extension_nonempty K ht (by
              calc
                2 * h ≤ 2 * J := by omega
                _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
                  (sideComplement_finrank I copies U A).symm))
            (transportedCenterTable I copies U A C)
            (transportedLeafTable I copies U A T) at hAvgBound
    exact hAvgBound
  have hqavg : successMargin E ≤
      ∑ A : SideComplement I copies U,
        ((1 : Rat) / Fintype.card (SideComplement I copies U)) * D A :=
    hscore.trans haverage
  let cardA : Nat := Fintype.card (SideComplement I copies U)
  have hcardPos : 0 < cardA := by
    dsimp [cardA]
    exact Fintype.card_pos_iff.mpr inferInstance
  have hcardNe : (cardA : Rat) ≠ 0 := by
    exact Nat.cast_ne_zero.mpr hcardPos.ne'
  have hscaled := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hqavg (Nat.cast_nonneg cardA)
  have hnormalize : (cardA : Rat) *
      (∑ A : SideComplement I copies U,
        ((1 : Rat) / (cardA : Rat)) * D A) =
      ∑ A : SideComplement I copies U, D A := by
    rw [Finset.mul_sum]
    apply Finset.sum_congr rfl
    intro A _
    field_simp [hcardNe]
  rw [hnormalize] at hscaled
  have hsumq :
      (∑ _A : SideComplement I copies U, successMargin E) =
        (cardA : Rat) * successMargin E := by
    simp [cardA, Finset.sum_const, nsmul_eq_mul]
  have hsumle :
      (∑ _A : SideComplement I copies U, successMargin E) ≤
        ∑ A : SideComplement I copies U, D A := by
    rw [hsumq]
    exact hscaled
  obtain ⟨A, hAmem, hA⟩ := Finset.exists_le_of_sum_le
    (s := (Finset.univ : Finset (SideComplement I copies U)))
    (f := fun _ : SideComplement I copies U => successMargin E)
    (g := D) Finset.univ_nonempty hsumle
  refine ⟨A, ?_⟩
  have hscoreA : successMargin E ≤ D A := hA
  have hdA : 2 * h ≤ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 := by
    rw [sideComplement_finrank I copies U A]
    omega
  have hscoreA' : successMargin E ≤
      StarDensity (V := A.1) (k := k)
        (grass_nonempty_of_le (by
          calc
            t ≤ 2 * h := ht
            _ ≤ 2 * J := by omega
            _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
              (sideComplement_finrank I copies U A).symm))
        (fun K => extension_nonempty K ht (by
          calc
            2 * h ≤ 2 * J := by omega
            _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
              (sideComplement_finrank I copies U A).symm))
        (transportedCenterTable I copies U A C)
        (transportedLeafTable I copies U A T) := by
    simpa [D] using hscoreA
  have hcoord := selected_coordinate_score_of_actual_complement_score
    I copies U A C T (by omega) hdA hscoreA'
  exact hcoord

/-- Local tracked copy of the manuscript's displayed rational-rho scale. -/
def localManuscriptSuccessScale (rho : Rat) (m h : Nat) : Real :=
  (1 / 2 : Real) ^ (2 * (1 - 1000 * (rho : Real)) *
    (h : Real) * (m : Real))

def localManuscriptRho (m : Nat) : Rat :=
  ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho m

/-- Divisibility by the actual reciprocal block parameter identifies this
displayed scale with the existing natural-exponent rational success margin. -/
theorem local_manuscript_scale_eq_successMargin {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 0 < m)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h) :
    localManuscriptSuccessScale (localManuscriptRho m) m h =
      (ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.successMargin
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) : Real) := by
  let q : Nat := h / ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m
  have hbpos : 0 < ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m := by
    dsimp [ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf]
    positivity
  have hbne : (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real) ≠ 0 := by
    exact_mod_cast (Nat.ne_of_gt hbpos)
  have hmul : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m * q = h := by
    dsimp [q]
    exact Nat.mul_div_cancel' hdiv
  have hb1000 : 1000 <= ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m := by
    dsimp [ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf]
    have hm1 : 1 <= m := by omega
    have hsquare : 1 <= m ^ 2 := Nat.one_le_pow 2 m hm1
    nlinarith
  have hsub : 1000 * q <= h := by
    calc
      1000 * q <= ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m * q :=
        Nat.mul_le_mul_right q hb1000
      _ = h := hmul
  have hcastmul : (h : Real) =
      (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real) * (q : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hmul.symm
  have hrho : (localManuscriptRho m : Real) =
      1 / (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real) := by
    change (ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho m : Real) = _
    have hrat := ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho_eq_inverse_bOf hm
    rw [hrat, Rat.cast_div]
    norm_num
  have hexp :
      2 * (1 - 1000 * (localManuscriptRho m : Real)) *
        (h : Real) * (m : Real) =
      (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h : Real) := by
    rw [hrho, hcastmul]
    have hcastsub : ((h - 1000 * q : Nat) : Real) =
        (h : Real) - 1000 * (q : Real) := by
      rw [Nat.cast_sub hsub, Nat.cast_mul]
      norm_num
    have hbadexp :
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h : Real) =
          2 * (m : Real) * ((h - 1000 * q : Nat) : Real) := by
      unfold ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent
      rw [show h / ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m = q from rfl]
      push_cast
      simp only [Nat.cast_sub hsub, Nat.cast_mul]
    rw [hbadexp, hcastsub, hcastmul]
    field_simp [hbne] <;> ring
  rw [localManuscriptSuccessScale, hexp, Real.rpow_natCast]
  simp [ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.successMargin, Rat.cast_pow]

def sourceRankExponent (m : Nat) : Nat := 40000 * m ^ 3

def sourceMomentTLowerBound (m : Nat) : Nat := sourceHolderLowerBound m

def sourceMomentT (m : Nat) : Nat := 2 ^ sourceMomentTLowerBound m

def sourceMomentP (m : Nat) : Nat := 2 ^ (m * sourceMomentT m)

/-- Explicit enlarged height used to pay for the finite HC constants at the
actual source Holder exponent P. It is allowed to be very large: the selected
height floor is a source cutoff, and no asymptotic shorthand replaces it. -/
def sourceLowHCHeightFloor (m : Nat) : Nat :=
  1000000 * m ^ 2 *
    (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m + 100)

/-- Exact base-two exponent of the low analytic term after division by the
same selected weighted-star signal, using beta's unconditional signal floor.
The count-rate term is the strengthened h/(1000*m^2) estimate. -/
def sourceLowRelativeExponent (m h P : Nat) : Real :=
  let mm : Real := m
  let hh : Real := h
  let pp : Real := P
  let rho : Real := fixedRho m
  let alpha : Real := 1 - 2 / pp
  let rate : Real :=
    1 / (1000 * mm) - 2 * mm * alpha * (1 - 1000 * rho ^ 2) +
      (mm / pp) *
        (2 * mm * (1 - 1000 * rho) + 2 * (1 - rho) + 2 * mm * rho) +
      2 * mm * (1 - 1000 * rho) + 2 * mm * rho
  let const : Real :=
    mm + mm * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 * pp + 2 * alpha) +
      (mm / pp) * 2 + 2
  const + rate * hh

/-- Exponent of the same selected beta floor `successScale/4 · M/F`, after
using the actual selected dimensions `c=2(1-rho)h` and `s=2rho h`. -/
def sourceLowBetaFloorExponent (m h : Nat) : Real :=
  -2 * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) * (m : Real) * (h : Real) - 2 -
    2 * (1 - (fixedRho m : Real)) * (h : Real) -
    2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) * (h : Real)

/-- Exponent of the selected first weighted-star signal, with its exact
`successScale/4 · M/B` factor retained. -/
def sourceLowTargetSignalExponent (m h : Nat) : Real :=
  -2 * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) * (m : Real) * (h : Real) - 2 -
    2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) * (h : Real)

/-- The beta-floor exponent is exactly the rational success scale, the `1/4`
selector factor, and the selected coordinate ratio `M/F`, with the actual
fixed-rho leaf dimensions substituted. This isolates the scalar identity
needed to feed the one-functional selector output into the low comparison. -/
theorem source_low_beta_floor_exponent_selected_dimensions {m h c s : Nat}
    {rho : Real}
    (hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = rho)
    (hc : (c : Real) = 2 * (1 - rho) * (h : Real))
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * rho * (h : Real)) :
    sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h =
      -2 * (1 - 1000 * rho) * (m : Real) * (h : Real) - 2 -
        (c : Real) - (m : Real) * (s : Real) := by
  unfold sourceLowBetaFloorExponent
  rw [hrho, hc, hs]
  ring

/-- The source integer bad exponent is the same real exponent as the displayed
fixed-rho success scale once the selected height is divisible by `bOf`. -/
theorem bad_exponent_cast_eq_fixed_rho_scale {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 0 < m)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h) :
    (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h : Real) =
      2 * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) * (m : Real) * (h : Real) := by
  let b := ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m
  let q := h / b
  have hbpos : 0 < b := by dsimp [b, ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf]; positivity
  have hbne : (b : Real) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (Nat.ne_of_gt hbpos)
  have hmul : b * q = h := by
    dsimp [q]
    exact Nat.mul_div_cancel' hdiv
  have hsub : 1000 * q ≤ h := by
    have hb1000 : 1000 ≤ b := by
      dsimp [b, ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf]
      have hm1 : 1 ≤ m := by omega
      nlinarith [Nat.one_le_pow 2 m hm1]
    calc
      1000 * q ≤ b * q := Nat.mul_le_mul_right q hb1000
      _ = h := hmul
  have hcastmul : (h : Real) = (b : Real) * (q : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hmul.symm
  have hcastsub : ((h - 1000 * q : Nat) : Real) =
      (h : Real) - 1000 * (q : Real) := by
    rw [Nat.cast_sub hsub, Nat.cast_mul]
    norm_num
  have hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = 1 / (b : Real) := by
    dsimp [b]
    have hrat := ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho_eq_inverse_bOf hm
    rw [hrat, Rat.cast_div]
    norm_num
  unfold ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent
  rw [show h / ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m = q from rfl]
  push_cast
  rw [hcastsub, hcastmul, hrho]
  field_simp [hbne] <;> ring

/-- Exact conversion of the selected rational `M/F` factor to its real
negative-exponent form. The natural subtraction is justified by the caller's
actual dimension guard. -/
theorem selected_M_over_F_eq_negative_power {n c s m : Nat}
    (hdim : c + m * s ≤ n) :
    ((((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s))) / (2 : Rat) ^ n : Rat) : Real) =
      (2 : Real) ^ (-((c : Real) + (m : Real) * (s : Real))) := by
  have hpow : (2 : Rat) ^ n =
      (2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) * (2 : Rat) ^ (c + m * s) := by
    rw [← pow_add, Nat.sub_add_cancel hdim]
  have hrat : (2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n =
      1 / (2 : Rat) ^ (c + m * s) := by
    rw [hpow]
    field_simp
  have hcast :
      ((((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s))) / (2 : Rat) ^ n : Rat) : Real) =
        1 / (2 : Real) ^ (c + m * s) := by
    have hcast' := congrArg (fun x : Rat => (x : Real)) hrat
    norm_num [Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_pow, Rat.cast_ofNat] at hcast'
    norm_num [Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_pow, Rat.cast_ofNat, one_div] at hcast' ⊢
    exact hcast'
  rw [hcast, one_div, ← Real.rpow_natCast (2 : Real) (c + m * s),
    ← Real.rpow_neg (by norm_num : (0 : Real) ≤ 2)]
  congr 1
  push_cast
  ring

/-- The exact selector beta signal has the exponent used by the analytic
low-term comparison when the source dimensions and fixed-rho scale are the
selected ones. -/
theorem selected_beta_signal_eq_source_floor {m h n c s E : Nat}
    (hm : 0 < m)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h)
    (hE : E = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
    (hdim : c + m * s ≤ n)
    {rho : Real}
    (hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = rho)
    (hc : (c : Real) = 2 * (1 - rho) * (h : Real))
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * rho * (h : Real)) :
    ((((successMargin E / 4) *
      ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n) : Rat) : Real)) =
      (2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h := by
  have hsuccess : (successMargin E : Real) =
      (2 : Real) ^ (-((ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h : Nat) : Real)) := by
    rw [hE]
    unfold successMargin
    rw [Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_pow]
    norm_num [Rat.cast_one, Rat.cast_ofNat]
  have hM := selected_M_over_F_eq_negative_power (n := n) (c := c)
    (s := s) (m := m) hdim
  rw [Rat.cast_div] at hM
  have hbad := bad_exponent_cast_eq_fixed_rho_scale hm hdiv
  rw [hrho] at hbad
  simp only [Rat.cast_mul, Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_ofNat]
  rw [hsuccess, hM]
  rw [div_eq_mul_inv,
    show (4 : Real)⁻¹ = (2 : Real) ^ (-2 : Real) by norm_num [Real.rpow_neg]]
  rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2),
    ← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2)]
  congr 1
  rw [hbad]
  rw [source_low_beta_floor_exponent_selected_dimensions hrho hc hs]
  ring

/-! The high-error comparison uses a conservative dyadic signal with the
success scale squared. This lemma compares that quantity to the actual
selected additive signal `successMargin E / 4 * M/F`; it does not identify
the two signals. -/
theorem selected_actual_additive_signal_dominates_dyadic
    {m h n c s E : Nat} (hm : 256 <= m)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h)
    (hE : E = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
    (hdim : c + m * s <= n) {rho : Real}
    (hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = rho)
    (hc : (c : Real) = 2 * (1 - rho) * (h : Real))
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * rho * (h : Real)) :
    (2 : Real) ^ (-4 * (1 - 1000 * rho) * (m : Real) * (h : Real) - 2 -
        (m : Real) * (s : Real)) <=
      ((((successMargin E / 4) *
        ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n : Rat) : Rat) : Real)) := by
  have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
  have hmR : (1 : Real) <= (m : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast (show 1 <= m by omega)
  have hmRlarge : (256 : Real) <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hm
  have hrhoSmallRat := fixedRho_le_one_div_4000 hmpos
  have hrhoSmall : (fixedRho m : Real) <= 1 / 4000 := by
    have hcast := (Rat.cast_le (K := Real)).mpr hrhoSmallRat
    norm_num at hcast ⊢
    exact hcast
  have hsignal := selected_beta_signal_eq_source_floor hmpos hdiv hE hdim
    hrho hc hs
  have hgap :
      -4 * (1 - 1000 * rho) * (m : Real) * (h : Real) - 2 -
          (m : Real) * (s : Real) <= sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h := by
    unfold sourceLowBetaFloorExponent
    rw [hs, hrho]
    have hcoef : 0 <= 1000 * (m : Real) - 1 := by nlinarith
    have hfactor : 0 <= (m : Real) - 1 -
        (fixedRho m : Real) * (1000 * (m : Real) - 1) := by
      have hbound := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hrhoSmall hcoef
      have hmRlarge : (256 : Real) <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hm
      have hbound' : (1 / 4000 : Real) * (1000 * (m : Real) - 1) <=
          (m : Real) - 1 := by nlinarith [hmRlarge]
      exact sub_nonneg.mpr (le_trans hbound hbound')
    have hscale : 0 <= 2 * (h : Real) := by positivity
    nlinarith [mul_nonneg hfactor hscale]
  have hpow := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le
    (by norm_num : (1 : Real) <= 2) hgap
  rw [hsignal]
  simpa using hpow

/-- The selector's first weighted term is exactly the dyadic low target after
the selected `M/B` ratio is expanded. This is the low-signal identity needed
to combine the Holder estimate with the *same* selector witness. -/
theorem selected_actual_first_signal_eq_dyadic
    {m h n c s E : Nat} (hm : 0 < m)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h)
    (hE : E = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
    (hdim : c + m * s <= n) {rho : Real}
    (hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = rho)
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * rho * (h : Real)) :
    ((((successMargin E / 4) *
      ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ (n - c)) : Rat) : Real)) =
      (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) := by
  have hsuccess : (successMargin E : Real) =
      (2 : Real) ^ (-((ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h : Nat) : Real)) := by
    rw [hE]
    unfold successMargin
    rw [Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_pow]
    norm_num [Rat.cast_one, Rat.cast_ofNat]
  have hnat : n - (c + m * s) + m * s = n - c := by omega
  have hratioRat :
      (2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ (n - c) =
        1 / (2 : Rat) ^ (m * s) := by
    rw [← hnat, pow_add]
    field_simp
  have hratio :
      ((((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ (n - c) : Rat) : Real)) =
        (2 : Real) ^ (-((m : Real) * (s : Real))) := by
    have hcast := congrArg (fun x : Rat => (x : Real)) hratioRat
    rw [Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_pow] at hcast
    norm_num [Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_pow, Rat.cast_ofNat, one_div] at hcast ⊢
    rw [hcast, ← Real.rpow_natCast (2 : Real) (m * s),
      ← Real.rpow_neg (by norm_num : (0 : Real) <= 2)]
    have hcastMul : ((m * s : Nat) : Real) = (m : Real) * (s : Real) := by
      exact_mod_cast Nat.cast_mul m s
    rw [hcastMul]
  have hbad := bad_exponent_cast_eq_fixed_rho_scale hm hdiv
  rw [hrho] at hbad
  have hexp :
      -((ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h : Nat) : Real) - 2 -
        (m : Real) * (s : Real) = sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h := by
    rw [hbad, hs]
    unfold sourceLowTargetSignalExponent
    rw [hrho]
    ring
  rw [Rat.cast_mul, Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_ofNat, hsuccess, hratio,
    div_eq_mul_inv]
  rw [show (4 : Real)⁻¹ = (2 : Real) ^ (-2 : Real) by norm_num [Real.rpow_neg]]
  rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2),
    ← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2)]
  have hexpAdd :
      -((ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h : Nat) : Real) +
          (-2) + (-((m : Real) * (s : Real))) =
        sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h := by
    nlinarith [hexp]
  rw [hexpAdd]

/-- The selected center atom is a probability under the actual shared-center
star law. This supplies the genuine `beta <= 1` guard required by the
high-error moment estimate. -/
theorem selected_matching_center_mass_le_one
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [Fintype V]
    {c s m : Nat} (hdV : c + s <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V)
    (C : ActualSourceStarLaw.CenterTable (V := V) c)
    (f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) V) :
    matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdV C f <= 1 := by
  classical
  unfold matchingCenterMass
  let mu := ActualSourceStarLaw.starLaw
    (V := V) (t := c) (d := c + s) (m := m)
      (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdV
  calc
    eventMass mu (Finset.univ.filter fun z : ActualSourceStarLaw.StarTuple
        (V := V) c (c + s) m =>
          f.comp z.1.val.subtype = C z.1) <= eventMass mu Finset.univ := by
      apply ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.eventMass_mono
      exact Finset.filter_subset _ _
    _ = 1 := ActualFiniteLaw.eventMass_univ mu

/-- The proved high-error budget is below half of the *actual* selected
additive signal. The intermediate dyadic scale is only a conservative lower
bound on `successMargin E / 4 · M/F`; the equality is supplied by the exact
selected-dimension beta identity, not by an assumed signal field. -/
theorem selected_actual_high_error_budget_le_actual_additive_half
    {m h n c E : Nat} (hm : 256 <= m)
    (hh : 20001 * m ^ 2 <= h)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h)
    (hE : E = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
    (hdim : c + m * ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h <= n)
    {rho : Real}
    (hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = rho)
    (hc : (c : Real) = 2 * (1 - rho) * (h : Real))
    (hs : (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h : Real) =
      2 * rho * (h : Real))
    {beta : Real} (hbeta0 : 0 <= beta) (hbeta1 : beta <= 1) :
    2 * ((2 : Real) * (2 : Real) ^
        (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) *
          ((ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h : Real) - 1)) /
          (ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticNumerics.manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 +
      (2 : Real) ^ (m : Real) *
        (ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticNumerics.manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ m * beta) <=
      ((((successMargin E / 4) *
        ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h)) /
          (2 : Rat) ^ n : Rat) : Rat) : Real)) / 2 := by
  have hbudget :=
    ActualSelectedHighErrorAdditiveSignal.doubled_high_error_budget_le_additive_half
      hm hh hdiv hbeta0 hbeta1
  have hsignal := selected_actual_additive_signal_dominates_dyadic
    hm hdiv hE hdim hrho hc hs
  have hsignal' :
      (2 : Real) ^
          (ActualSelectedHighErrorAdditiveSignal.sourceAdditiveSignalExponent m h) <=
        ((((successMargin E / 4) *
          ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h)) /
             (2 : Rat) ^ n : Rat) : Rat) : Real)) := by
    simpa [ActualSelectedHighErrorAdditiveSignal.sourceAdditiveSignalExponent, hrho]
      using hsignal
  exact hbudget.trans
    (div_le_div_of_nonneg_right hsignal' (by norm_num : (0 : Real) <= 2))

/-- The exact last scalar step in the same-functional inverse.  The low
term has already paid for the outer factor two; the high term consumes at
most half of the selector's additive signal.  Since that additive signal is
strictly positive, the two retained selector terms give a strict upper bound
on the same matching-star mass. -/
theorem selected_total_analytic_budget_contradicts_signal
    {X first additive low high : Real}
    (hselector : first + additive <= X)
    (hadditive : 0 < additive)
    (hlow : 2 * low <= first)
    (hhigh : 2 * high <= additive / 2)
    (hmass : X <= 2 * (low + high)) : False := by
  have htotal : 2 * (low + high) <= first + additive / 2 := by
    calc
      2 * (low + high) = 2 * low + 2 * high := by ring
      _ <= first + additive / 2 := add_le_add hlow hhigh
  have hstrict : first + additive / 2 < X := by linarith [hselector, hadditive]
  linarith [hmass, htotal]

/-- Exponent of the dyadic upper envelope for the low HC coefficient. The
factor four is the honest rational-threshold rounding bound. -/
def sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent (m h P : Nat) : Real :=
  (h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) +
    500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 * (P : Real) +
    (1 - 2 / (P : Real)) *
      (2 - 2 * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) * (h : Real))

/-- The explicit logarithmic exponent in `sourceLowRelativeExponent` is the
exact exponent of the low coefficient after beta-floor loss, divided by the
same selected weighted-star signal. This identity keeps the same P, rho and
selected dimensions in the arithmetic. -/
theorem source_low_relative_exponent_is_signal_ratio_exponent
    {m h P : Nat} (hm : 0 < m) (hP : 0 < P) :
    sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P =
      (m : Real) + (m : Real) * sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P -
        ((m : Real) / (P : Real)) * sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h -
        sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h := by
  have hmR : (m : Real) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (Nat.ne_of_gt hm)
  have hPR : (P : Real) ≠ 0 := by exact_mod_cast (Nat.ne_of_gt hP)
  unfold sourceLowRelativeExponent sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent
    sourceLowBetaFloorExponent sourceLowTargetSignalExponent
  dsimp
  field_simp [hmR, hPR]
  ring

/-- Exponentiating the identity above gives the exact dyadic ratio that is
controlled by the low-HC cutoff. The outer factor two in the eventual star
mass comparison is retained by the separate `1 + exponent` bound below. -/
theorem source_low_dyadic_ratio_eq_exponent
    {m h P : Nat} (hm : 0 < m) (hP : 0 < P) :
    (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P) =
      (2 : Real) ^ (m : Real) *
        ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
          (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
      ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^
          (m : Real) /
        ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h)) := by
  rw [source_low_relative_exponent_is_signal_ratio_exponent hm hP]
  rw [← Real.rpow_mul (by norm_num : (0 : Real) <= 2),
    ← Real.rpow_mul (by norm_num : (0 : Real) <= 2),
    ← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2),
    ← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2),
    ← Real.rpow_sub (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2)]
  congr 1
  ring

/- Attempt to discharge the low contribution against the first weighted
signal term. The exponent includes the same-f beta lower floor, the actual
failed-zoom agreement exponent, the factor-four rational tolerance, and the
finite rank-count loss. -/
set_option maxHeartbeats 800000 in
theorem source_low_relative_exponent_le_neg_one {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h) :
    sourceLowRelativeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m) <= -1 := by
  have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
  have hmR : (256 : Real) <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hm
  have hrho := fixed_rho_explicit hmpos
  have hT : sourceHolderLowerBound m <= sourceMomentT m := by
    unfold sourceMomentT
    exact (Nat.lt_two_pow_self (n := sourceHolderLowerBound m)).le
  have hPpow : m * sourceMomentT m <= sourceMomentP m := by
    unfold sourceMomentP
    exact (Nat.lt_two_pow_self (n := m * sourceMomentT m)).le
  have hPbase : 16000 * m ^ 2 * (m + 3) <= sourceMomentP m := by
    calc
      16000 * m ^ 2 * (m + 3) = m * sourceHolderLowerBound m := by
        simp [sourceHolderLowerBound]
        ring
      _ <= m * sourceMomentT m := Nat.mul_le_mul_left m hT
      _ <= sourceMomentP m := hPpow
  have hPbaseR : 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 * ((m + 3 : Nat) : Real) <=
      (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hPbase
  have hmPlus3 : ((m + 3 : Nat) : Real) = (m : Real) + 3 := by
    norm_num
  rw [hmPlus3] at hPbaseR
  have hfloorR : 1000000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 *
      (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
        (sourceMomentP m : Real) + 100) <= (h : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hfloor
  have hmRpos : 0 < (m : Real) := by positivity
  have hM3 : 1 <= (m : Real) + 3 := by linarith
  have hmleM3 : (m : Real) <= (m : Real) + 3 := by linarith
  have hPbound : 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 <= (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    have hmul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hM3
      (show 0 <= 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 by positivity)
    have hmul' : 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 <=
        16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 * ((m : Real) + 3) := by
      simpa [mul_one] using hmul
    exact hmul'.trans hPbaseR
  have hPboundStrong : 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 3 <=
      (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    have hmul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hmleM3
      (show 0 <= 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 by positivity)
    have hmul' : 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 3 <=
        16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 * ((m : Real) + 3) := by
      have hmPow : (m : Real) ^ 3 = (m : Real) ^ 2 * (m : Real) := by ring
      rw [hmPow]
      simpa [mul_assoc] using hmul
    exact hmul'.trans hPbaseR
  have hPpos : 0 < (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    have hpn : 0 < sourceMomentP m := by
      unfold sourceMomentP
      exact Nat.pow_pos (by omega)
    exact_mod_cast hpn
  have hPsmall : (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) <=
      1 / (16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) := by
    rw [div_le_iff₀ hPpos]
    field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos]
    nlinarith [hPboundStrong]
  have h4mP : 4 * (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) <=
      1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := by
    rw [div_le_iff₀ hPpos]
    field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos]
    have hm1 : 1 <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 <= m by omega)
    nlinarith [hPbound, hm1]
  have hbetaRate : (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) *
      (2 * (m : Real) + 2) <= 1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := by
    have hbracket : 2 * (m : Real) + 2 <= 4 * (m : Real) := by
      have hm1 : 1 <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 <= m by omega)
      linarith
    have hscaled := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hbracket
      (show 0 <= (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) by positivity)
    have hsmall' := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hPsmall
      (show 0 <= 4 * (m : Real) by positivity)
    have hsmallEq :
        1 / (16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) * (4 * (m : Real)) =
          1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := by
      field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos]
      ring
    have hm1 : 1 <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast (show 1 <= m by omega)
    calc
      (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * (2 * (m : Real) + 2) <=
          (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * (4 * (m : Real)) := hscaled
      _ <= 1 / (16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) * (4 * (m : Real)) := hsmall'
      _ = 1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := hsmallEq
  have hrhoEq : (fixedRho m : Real) = 1 / (4000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) := hrho
  have hsmallRho : 0 <= 1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 := by
    rw [hrhoEq]
    field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos]
    have hm1 : 1 <= m := by omega
    have hm4Nat : 1 ^ 4 <= m ^ 4 := Nat.pow_le_pow_left hm1 4
    have hm4 : (1 : Real) <= (m : Real) ^ 4 := by exact_mod_cast hm4Nat
    nlinarith [hm4]
  have hmain :
      -2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
          (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) +
        2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
        2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) <=
      -1998 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) +
        2000 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 +
        4 * (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    have hEq :
        -2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
            (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) +
          2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
          2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) =
        -1998 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) +
          2000 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 +
          4 * (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) -
          4000 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 /
            (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
      field_simp [ne_of_gt hPpos]
      ring
    rw [hEq]
    have hloss : 0 <= 4000 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 /
        (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by positivity
    linarith [hloss]
  have hrhoSq : 2000 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 <=
      1 / (8000 * (m : Real)) := by
    rw [hrhoEq]
    have hm2 : 1 <= (m : Real) ^ 2 := by nlinarith [hmR]
    field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos]
    nlinarith [hm2]
  have hrate :
      1 / (1000 * (m : Real)) -
        2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
          (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) +
        (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) *
          (2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
            2 * (1 - (fixedRho m : Real)) +
            2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
        2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
        2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) <= -(1 / (3 * (m : Real))) := by
    have hnegRho : -1998 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) =
        -1998 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := by
      rw [hrhoEq]
      field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos]
    have hmainBound :
        -2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
            (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) +
          2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
          2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) <=
        -1998 / (4000 * (m : Real)) +
          1 / (8000 * (m : Real)) + 1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := by
      calc
        _ <= -1998 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) +
            2000 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 +
            4 * (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) := hmain
        _ = -1998 / (4000 * (m : Real)) +
            2000 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2 +
            4 * (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by rw [hnegRho]
        _ <= -1998 / (4000 * (m : Real)) +
            1 / (8000 * (m : Real)) + 1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := by
              nlinarith [hrhoSq, h4mP]
    have hbracket : 2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
        2 * (1 - (fixedRho m : Real)) +
        2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) <= 2 * (m : Real) + 2 := by
      have hrho0 : 0 <= (fixedRho m : Real) := by rw [hrhoEq]; positivity
      nlinarith
    have hbetaterm := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hbracket
      (show 0 <= (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) by positivity)
    have hbetaBound :
        (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) *
          (2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
            2 * (1 - (fixedRho m : Real)) +
            2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real)) <=
          1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := hbetaterm.trans hbetaRate
    have hsumBound :
        1 / (1000 * (m : Real)) +
          (-1998 / (4000 * (m : Real)) +
            1 / (8000 * (m : Real)) + 1 / (4000 * (m : Real))) +
          1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) <= -(1 / (3 * (m : Real))) := by
      have hcoeff : (1 / 1000 : Real) - 1998 / 4000 +
          1 / 8000 + 1 / 4000 + 1 / 4000 <= -(1 / 3 : Real) := by
        norm_num
      calc
        _ = (1 / (m : Real)) *
            (1 / 1000 - 1998 / 4000 + 1 / 8000 + 1 / 4000 +
              1 / 4000) := by
                field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos] <;> ring
        _ <= (1 / (m : Real)) * (-(1 / 3 : Real)) :=
          mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hcoeff (by positivity)
        _ = -(1 / (3 * (m : Real))) := by
          field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos]
    calc
      _ = 1 / (1000 * (m : Real)) +
          ((-2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
              (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) +
            2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
            2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
            (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) *
              (2 * (m : Real) * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) +
                2 * (1 - (fixedRho m : Real)) +
                2 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real))) := by ring
      _ <= 1 / (1000 * (m : Real)) +
          (-1998 / (4000 * (m : Real)) +
            1 / (8000 * (m : Real)) + 1 / (4000 * (m : Real))) +
          1 / (4000 * (m : Real)) := by
            nlinarith [hmainBound, hbetaBound]
      _ <= -(1 / (3 * (m : Real))) := hsumBound
  have hconst :
      (m : Real) +
        (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
          (sourceMomentP m : Real) +
          2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
        (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2 + 2 <=
      (h : Real) / (3 * (m : Real)) - 1 := by
    have halphaUpper :
        1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real) <= 1 := by
      have hrecip : 0 <= 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by positivity
      linarith
    have hratio : (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) <= (m : Real) := by
      have hPone : (1 : Real) <= (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
        have hbaseOne : (1 : Real) <=
            16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 * ((m : Real) + 3) := by
          have hA : (1 : Real) <= 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 := by
            nlinarith [hmR]
          have hB : (1 : Real) <= (m : Real) + 3 := by linarith [hmR]
          have hA0 : (0 : Real) <= 16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 :=
            le_trans (by norm_num) hA
          calc
            (1 : Real) = 1 * 1 := by ring
            _ <= (16000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) * ((m : Real) + 3) :=
              mul_le_mul hA hB (by norm_num) hA0
        exact hbaseOne.trans hPbaseR
      have hmP : (m : Real) <= (m : Real) * (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
        calc
          (m : Real) = (m : Real) * 1 := by ring
          _ <= (m : Real) * (sourceMomentP m : Real) :=
            mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hPone (by positivity)
      exact (div_le_iff₀ hPpos).2 hmP
    have hfloor' : 3 * (m : Real) *
        ((m : Real) + (m : Real) *
          (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
          (sourceMomentP m : Real) + 4) + 3) <= (h : Real) := by
      nlinarith [hfloorR, hmR]
    have hconstPlusOne :
        (m : Real) +
          (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
            (sourceMomentP m : Real) +
            2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
          (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2 + 3 <=
        (h : Real) / (3 * (m : Real)) := by
      have halphaInner : 2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) <= 2 := by
        linarith [halphaUpper]
      have halphaScaled := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left halphaInner
        (show 0 <= (m : Real) by positivity)
      have hratioScaled := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hratio
        (show 0 <= (2 : Real) by norm_num)
      have hsmallTerms :
          (m : Real) * (2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
            (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2 <=
          (m : Real) * 4 := by
        calc
          _ <= (m : Real) * 2 + (m : Real) * 2 :=
            add_le_add halphaScaled hratioScaled
          _ = (m : Real) * 4 := by ring
      have hsmallTotal :
          (m : Real) * (2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
              (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2 <=
            (m : Real) * 4 := hsmallTerms
      have hinside :
          (m : Real) +
              (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
                (sourceMomentP m : Real) +
                2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
              (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2 + 3 <=
            (m : Real) +
              (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
                (sourceMomentP m : Real) + 4) + 3 := by
        calc
          (m : Real) +
                (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
                  (sourceMomentP m : Real) +
                  2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
                (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2 + 3
              = (m : Real) +
                (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
                  (sourceMomentP m : Real)) +
                ((m : Real) * (2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
                  (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2) + 3 := by ring
          _ <= (m : Real) +
                (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
                  (sourceMomentP m : Real)) + (m : Real) * 4 + 3 := by
                gcongr
          _ = (m : Real) +
                (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
                  (sourceMomentP m : Real) + 4) + 3 := by ring
      have hbaseDiv :
          (m : Real) +
              (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
                (sourceMomentP m : Real) + 4) + 3 <=
            (h : Real) / (3 * (m : Real)) := by
        apply (le_div_iff₀
          (by positivity : (0 : Real) < 3 * (m : Real))).2
        simpa only [mul_comm] using hfloor'
      exact hinside.trans hbaseDiv
    have hplus :
        ((m : Real) +
          (m : Real) * (500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 *
            (sourceMomentP m : Real) +
            2 * (1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real))) +
          (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) * 2 + 2) + 1 <=
        ((h : Real) / (3 * (m : Real)) - 1) + 1 := by
      convert hconstPlusOne using 1 <;> ring
    exact (add_le_add_iff_right (1 : Real)).mp hplus
  have hrateMul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hrate
    (show 0 <= (h : Real) by positivity)
  rw [hrho] at hrateMul
  have hcombined := add_le_add hconst hrateMul
  have hcancel : (h : Real) / (3 * (m : Real)) +
      (-(1 / (3 * (m : Real))) * (h : Real)) = 0 := by
    field_simp [ne_of_gt (show (0 : Real) < 3 * (m : Real) by positivity)]
    ring
  unfold sourceLowRelativeExponent
  rw [hrho]
  dsimp
  calc
    _ <= ((h : Real) / (3 * (m : Real)) - 1) +
        (-(1 / (3 * (m : Real))) * (h : Real)) := by
          simpa [sourceLowRelativeExponent, hrho] using hcombined
    _ = -1 := by nlinarith [hcancel]

/-- The explicit coefficient and height estimates make the low-term ratio
at most one after exponentiation. This follows from the derived cutoff. -/
theorem source_low_relative_factor_with_outer_two_le_one {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h) :
    (2 : Real) ^ (1 + sourceLowRelativeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m)) <= 1 := by
  have hexp := source_low_relative_exponent_le_neg_one hm hfloor
  have hsum : 1 + sourceLowRelativeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m) <= 0 := by linarith
  have hpow := Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le
    (by norm_num : (1 : Real) <= 2) hsum
  simpa using hpow

/-- Monotonicity of a negative Holder power, stated here before its first
use so the low-term comparison has no forward declaration dependency. -/
theorem selected_beta_negative_holder_power_le_signal_early
    {beta signal q : Real} (hsignal : 0 < signal)
    (hbeta : signal <= beta) (hq : 0 <= q) :
    beta ^ (-q) <= signal ^ (-q) := by
  have hbetaPos : 0 < beta := lt_of_lt_of_le hsignal hbeta
  have hpow := Real.rpow_le_rpow (le_of_lt hsignal) hbeta hq
  have hsignalPow : 0 < signal ^ q := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hsignal q
  have hbetaPow : 0 < beta ^ q := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hbetaPos q
  rw [Real.rpow_neg (le_of_lt hbetaPos), Real.rpow_neg (le_of_lt hsignal)]
  exact (inv_le_inv₀ hbetaPow hsignalPow).mpr hpow

/-- Compose the low analytic term with the same selected beta-weighted signal.
The two premises are the actual HC-envelope estimate and the actual
same-functional beta floor; the conclusion includes the outer factor two from
the selected star-mass comparison. -/
theorem selected_low_analytic_term_le_same_functional_signal
    {m h P : Nat} {beta low : Real}
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h)
    (hP : 0 < P) (hPeq : P = sourceMomentP m)
    (hbeta : (2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h <= beta)
    (hlow0 : 0 <= low)
    (hlow : low <= (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) :
    (2 : Real) ^ (m + 1) * beta ^ (1 - (m : Real) / (P : Real)) * low ^ m <=
      (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) * beta := by
  have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
  have hq : 0 <= (m : Real) / (P : Real) := by positivity
  have hbeta0 : 0 < (2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h :=
    Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _
  have hbetaPos : 0 < beta := lt_of_lt_of_le hbeta0 hbeta
  have hbetaNeg := selected_beta_negative_holder_power_le_signal_early
    hbeta0 hbeta hq
  have hcoeff0 : 0 <=
      (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P) := by positivity
  have hlowPow : low ^ m <=
      ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m := by
    have hr := Real.rpow_le_rpow hlow0 hlow (show 0 <= (m : Real) by positivity)
    simpa only [Real.rpow_natCast] using hr
  have hbetaSplit : beta ^ (1 - (m : Real) / (P : Real)) =
      beta * beta ^ (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) := by
    rw [show 1 - (m : Real) / (P : Real) =
      1 + (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) by ring,
      Real.rpow_add hbetaPos]
    simp
  have hcore :
      (2 : Real) ^ m * beta ^ (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) * low ^ m <=
        (2 : Real) ^ m *
          ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
            (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
          ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m := by
    have hbetaNeg0 : 0 <=
        ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
          (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) := by positivity
    have hlowPow0 : 0 <= low ^ m := pow_nonneg hlow0 m
    have hprod := mul_le_mul hbetaNeg hlowPow hlowPow0 hbetaNeg0
    calc
      (2 : Real) ^ m * beta ^ (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) * low ^ m =
          (2 : Real) ^ m *
            (beta ^ (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) * low ^ m) := by ring
      _ <= (2 : Real) ^ m *
          (((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
            (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
            ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m) :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hprod (by positivity)
      _ = (2 : Real) ^ m *
          ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
            (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
            ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m := by ring
  have hsignalPos : 0 <
      (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) :=
    Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _
  have hratio := source_low_dyadic_ratio_eq_exponent
    (m := m) (h := h) (P := P) hmpos hP
  have hratioMul := congrArg
    (fun x : Real => x * (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h)) hratio
  have hratioMul' :
      (2 : Real) ^ m *
          ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
            (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
          ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m =
        (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P) *
          (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) := by
    have hcancel := hratioMul
    rw [div_mul_cancel₀ _ (ne_of_gt hsignalPos)] at hcancel
    simpa only [Real.rpow_natCast] using hcancel.symm
  have houter0 := source_low_relative_factor_with_outer_two_le_one hm hfloor
  have houter : (2 : Real) ^ (1 + sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P) <= 1 := by
    simpa [hPeq] using houter0
  have houterSignal := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right houter
    (le_of_lt hsignalPos)
  have hratioOuter :
      2 * ((2 : Real) ^ m *
          ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
            (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
          ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m) <=
        (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) := by
    have hpow : (2 : Real) ^ (1 + sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P) =
        2 * (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P) := by
        rw [Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2)]
        simp
    calc
      _ = 2 * ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P) *
          (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h)) := by
            rw [hratioMul']
      _ = (2 : Real) ^ (1 + sourceLowRelativeExponent m h P) *
          (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) := by rw [hpow]; ring
      _ <= (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) := by
        simpa only [one_mul] using houterSignal
  have hscaledCore := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hcore
    (show 0 <= 2 * beta by positivity)
  have hbetaOuter := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hratioOuter
    (by exact le_of_lt hbetaPos)
  calc
    (2 : Real) ^ (m + 1) * beta ^ (1 - (m : Real) / (P : Real)) * low ^ m =
        beta * (2 * ((2 : Real) ^ m *
          beta ^ (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) * low ^ m)) := by
            rw [hbetaSplit]
            rw [pow_succ]
            ring
    _ <= beta * (2 * ((2 : Real) ^ m *
        ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
          (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
        ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m)) := by
          calc
            _ = (2 * beta) * ((2 : Real) ^ m *
                beta ^ (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) * low ^ m) := by ring
            _ <= (2 * beta) * ((2 : Real) ^ m *
                ((2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h) ^
                  (-((m : Real) / (P : Real))) *
                ((2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h P)) ^ m) :=
                  hscaledCore
            _ = _ := by ring
    _ <= beta * (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) := hbetaOuter
    _ = (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) * beta := by ring

/-- Independent cutoff used by the high spectral-tail component. The low-HC
rounding and coefficient budget is handled by `sourceLowHCHeightFloor`. -/
def sourceTailHeightFloor (m : Nat) : Nat := max (4000 * m ^ 2) (12 * m)

def sourceMarginHeightFloor (m : Nat) : Nat :=
  max (max (sourceTailHeightFloor m) (20001 * m ^ 2))
    (sourceLowHCHeightFloor m)

/-- The enlarged low-HC cutoff includes room for the factor-two rational
rounding loss after the moment power is taken. -/
theorem low_HC_height_floor_covers_rounding {m : Nat} (hm : 0 < m) :
    2 * m <= sourceLowHCHeightFloor m := by
  have hm1 : 1 <= m := Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.ne_of_gt hm)
  have hmSq : m <= m ^ 2 := by nlinarith
  have hfactor : 1 <= 500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 *
      sourceMomentP m + 100 := by omega
  have hprod0 := Nat.mul_le_mul_left (m ^ 2) hfactor
  have hprod : m ^ 2 <= m ^ 2 *
      (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m + 100) := by
    simpa [Nat.mul_one] using hprod0
  have hscaled : 2 * m <= 1000000 * m ^ 2 := by nlinarith
  unfold sourceLowHCHeightFloor
  calc
    2 * m <= 1000000 * m ^ 2 := hscaled
    _ <= 1000000 * (m ^ 2 *
        (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m + 100)) :=
          Nat.mul_le_mul_left 1000000 hprod
    _ = 1000000 * m ^ 2 *
        (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m + 100) := by
          ring

theorem low_HC_height_floor_dominates_m_squared {m : Nat} (hm : 0 < m) :
    (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 <= sourceLowHCHeightFloor m := by
  have hm1 : 1 <= m := Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.ne_of_gt hm)
  have hm2 : m ^ 2 <= m ^ 2 * m := by
    simpa using Nat.mul_le_mul_left (m ^ 2) hm1
  have hcoef : m ^ 2 * m <= 40000 * (m ^ 2 * m) :=
    by simpa using Nat.mul_le_mul_right (m ^ 2 * m) (by decide : 1 <= 40000)
  have hr : m ^ 2 <= sourceRankExponent m := by
    dsimp [sourceRankExponent]
    simpa [pow_succ, Nat.mul_assoc] using hm2.trans hcoef
  have hr4 : m ^ 4 <= sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 := by
    calc
      m ^ 4 = m ^ 2 * m ^ 2 := by ring
      _ <= sourceRankExponent m * sourceRankExponent m :=
        Nat.mul_le_mul hr hr
      _ = sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 := by ring
  have hm4 : m ^ 2 <= m ^ 4 := by
    have hsq : 1 <= m ^ 2 := Nat.one_le_pow 2 m hm1
    calc
      m ^ 2 = m ^ 2 * 1 := by simp
      _ <= m ^ 2 * m ^ 2 := Nat.mul_le_mul_left (m ^ 2) hsq
      _ = m ^ 4 := by ring
  have hbase : 16 * m ^ 2 <= 500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 := by
    calc
      16 * m ^ 2 <= 500 * m ^ 4 := by nlinarith [hm4]
      _ <= 500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 :=
        Nat.mul_le_mul_left 500 hr4
  have hP : 1 <= sourceMomentP m := Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr
    (Nat.ne_of_gt (Nat.pow_pos (by omega)))
  have hfactor : 16 * m ^ 2 <=
      500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m + 100 := by
    have hscaled := Nat.mul_le_mul_left (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2) hP
    calc
      16 * m ^ 2 <= 500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 := hbase
      _ <= 500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m := by
        simpa [Nat.mul_assoc] using hscaled
      _ <= 500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m + 100 :=
        Nat.le_add_right _ _
  have hprod := Nat.mul_le_mul_left (1000000 * m ^ 2) hfactor
  calc
    (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 = 16000000 * m ^ 4 := by ring
    _ = 1000000 * m ^ 2 * (16 * m ^ 2) := by ring
    _ <= 1000000 * m ^ 2 *
        (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m + 100) := hprod
    _ = sourceLowHCHeightFloor m := by
      simp [sourceLowHCHeightFloor, Nat.mul_assoc]

/-- The finite rank count costs only a square-root exponent. This is the
useful count estimate for comparing the actual HC sum to the weighted signal. -/
theorem low_level_count_le_sqrt_dyadic (h : Nat) :
    2 * h + 1 <= 2 ^ (2 * (Nat.sqrt h + 1)) := by
  let k := Nat.sqrt h
  have hrough : h <= k ^ 2 + k + k := by
    simpa [k, pow_two, Nat.mul_comm, Nat.add_assoc] using Nat.sqrt_le_add h
  have hcount : 2 * h + 1 <= 2 * (k + 1) ^ 2 + 1 := by
    dsimp [k] at hrough ⊢
    nlinarith [hrough]
  have hpow := Nat.two_mul_sq_add_one_le_two_pow_two_mul (k + 1)
  simpa [Nat.mul_add, Nat.mul_comm, Nat.mul_left_comm, Nat.mul_assoc] using
    hcount.trans hpow

theorem low_level_count_exponent_le_height_rate {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 0 < m) (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h) :
    (2 * (Nat.sqrt h + 1) : Real) <=
      (h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) := by
  have hlarge : (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 <= h :=
    (low_HC_height_floor_dominates_m_squared hm).trans hfloor
  have hroot : 4000 * m ^ 2 <= Nat.sqrt h := (Nat.le_sqrt').2 hlarge
  have hrootSq : Nat.sqrt h * Nat.sqrt h <= h := Nat.sqrt_le h
  have hsqrt1 : 1 <= Nat.sqrt h := by
    have hm1 : 1 <= m := Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr (Nat.ne_of_gt hm)
    have hmSq : 1 <= m ^ 2 := Nat.one_le_pow 2 m hm1
    have hcoef : 4000 <= 4000 * m ^ 2 := Nat.mul_le_mul_left 4000 hmSq
    omega
  have hsum : Nat.sqrt h + 1 <= 2 * Nat.sqrt h := by omega
  have hstep1 : 2000 * m ^ 2 * (Nat.sqrt h + 1) <=
      4000 * m ^ 2 * Nat.sqrt h := by
    calc
      2000 * m ^ 2 * (Nat.sqrt h + 1) <=
          2000 * m ^ 2 * (2 * Nat.sqrt h) :=
        Nat.mul_le_mul_left _ hsum
      _ = 4000 * m ^ 2 * Nat.sqrt h := by ring
  have hstep2 : 4000 * m ^ 2 * Nat.sqrt h <=
      Nat.sqrt h * Nat.sqrt h := Nat.mul_le_mul_right _ hroot
  have hnat : 2000 * m ^ 2 * (Nat.sqrt h + 1) <= h :=
    hstep1.trans (hstep2.trans hrootSq)
  rw [le_div_iff₀ (by positivity : (0 : Real) < 1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2)]
  have hnatR : 2000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 * ((Nat.sqrt h + 1 : Nat) : Real) <=
      (h : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hnat
  have hnatR' : 2000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 * ((Nat.sqrt h : Real) + 1) <=
      (h : Real) := by
    simpa [Nat.cast_add] using hnatR
  nlinarith [hnatR']

/-- The actual double-exponential sampler has enough blocks to dominate the
selected squared height whenever its fixed integer multiplier is at least one.
This proves the ambient growth guard from the sampler definition. -/
theorem selected_height_square_lt_blocks {A h : Nat} (hA : 1 <= A) :
    h ^ 2 < SamplerParameters.blocks A h := by
  have hsq : h ^ 2 <= A * h ^ 2 := by
    simpa using Nat.mul_le_mul_right (h ^ 2) hA
  exact hsq.trans_lt (SamplerParameters.numerator_lt_blocks A h)

/-- The selector's actual block height satisfies the sampler growth guard.
The selector certificate is retained here so the inequality is attached to
the same selected h used by the leaf dimensions and spectral contracts. -/
theorem selected_actual_height_square_lt_blocks
    (base : Nat -> Nat) (cutoff : Real -> Nat) {L m A : Nat}
    (hsel : ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector
      (fun j => max (ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
        base cutoff j) (j + 2)) L =
        (m : WithBot Nat)) (hA : 1 <= A) :
    (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) ^ 2 <
      SamplerParameters.blocks A
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) := by
  have hselected := ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.selected_spectral_parameters
    base cutoff hsel
  exact selected_height_square_lt_blocks hA

def sourceHighErrorExponentGap (m h : Nat) : Real :=
  (((8 : Real) / 3) * (m : Real) - 2 +
      (3996 * (m : Real) + 2) * (fixedRho m : Real)) * (h : Real) -
    ((sourceRankExponent m : Real) + 7)

def sourceHighErrorExponent (m h : Nat) : Real :=
  (sourceRankExponent m : Real) + 2 -
    ((20 : Real) / 3) * (m : Real) * (h : Real)

def sourceWeightedSignalExponent (m h : Nat) : Real :=
  -4 * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real)) * (m : Real) * (h : Real) - 5 -
    2 * (1 - (fixedRho m : Real)) * (h : Real) -
    4 * (m : Real) * (fixedRho m : Real) * (h : Real)

theorem source_signal_exponent_gap_exact {m h : Nat} :
    sourceWeightedSignalExponent m h - sourceHighErrorExponent m h =
      sourceHighErrorExponentGap m h := by
  unfold sourceWeightedSignalExponent sourceHighErrorExponent
    sourceHighErrorExponentGap sourceRankExponent
  ring

/-- The finite low-level HC coefficient is bounded by its worst rank term
times the number of available ranks. This keeps the actual rank-indexed sum
visible for the later source-height comparison. -/
theorem selected_low_HC46_constant_sum_bound {d r p : Nat} {eta : Real}
    (heta : 0 <= eta) :
    selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := d) (r := r) (p := p) eta <=
      ((d + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * r ^ 2 * p) *
          eta ^ (((p : Real) - 2) / (p : Real)) := by
  classical
  let S := selectedLowIndexSet d r
  let alpha : Real := ((p : Real) - 2) / (p : Real)
  let C : Real := (2 : Real) ^ (500 * r ^ 2 * p) * eta ^ alpha
  have hterm (i : Nat) (hi : i ∈ S) :
      (2 : Real) ^ (500 * i ^ 2 * p) * eta ^ alpha <= C := by
    have hir : i <= r := (Finset.mem_filter.mp hi).2
    have hi2 : i ^ 2 <= r ^ 2 := by
      calc
        i ^ 2 = i * i := by ring
        _ <= r * r := Nat.mul_le_mul hir hir
        _ = r ^ 2 := by ring
    have hexp : 500 * i ^ 2 * p <= 500 * r ^ 2 * p :=
      Nat.mul_le_mul_right p (Nat.mul_le_mul_left 500 hi2)
    have hpow : (2 : Real) ^ (500 * i ^ 2 * p) <=
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * r ^ 2 * p) :=
      pow_le_pow_right₀ (by norm_num) hexp
    have hetaPow : 0 <= eta ^ alpha := Real.rpow_nonneg heta _
    dsimp [C, alpha]
    exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hpow hetaPow
  have hsum :
      Finset.sum S (fun i => (2 : Real) ^ (500 * i ^ 2 * p) * eta ^ alpha) <=
        (S.card : Real) * C := by
    have hs := Finset.sum_le_card_nsmul S
      (fun i => (2 : Real) ^ (500 * i ^ 2 * p) * eta ^ alpha) C hterm
    simpa [nsmul_eq_mul] using hs
  have hcard : S.card <= d + 1 := by
    calc
      S.card <= (Finset.range (d + 1)).card :=
        Finset.card_le_card (Finset.filter_subset _ _)
      _ = d + 1 := Finset.card_range (d + 1)
  have hcardR : (S.card : Real) <= ((d + 1 : Nat) : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hcard
  unfold selectedLowHC46NormBound
  calc
    Finset.sum (selectedLowIndexSet d r)
        (fun i => (2 : Real) ^ (500 * i ^ 2 * p) * eta ^ (((p : Real) - 2) / (p : Real)))
        <= (S.card : Real) * C := by simpa [S, alpha, C] using hsum
    _ <= ((d + 1 : Nat) : Real) * C :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcardR (by positivity)
    _ = ((d + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * r ^ 2 * p) *
          eta ^ (((p : Real) - 2) / (p : Real)) := by
      dsimp [C]
      ring


/-- The actual agreement-scale finite coefficient estimate follows from
`selected_low_HC46_constant_sum_bound`; the later margin comparison retains
the beta weight and is not asserted by this component. -/
theorem selected_actual_low_HC_sum_bound {h m : Nat} :
    selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := 2 * h) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
      (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) <=
      ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
          (2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
            (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) := by
  have heta : 0 <= 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h := by
    unfold manuscriptAgreementFloor
    positivity
  simpa using selected_low_HC46_constant_sum_bound
    (d := 2 * h) (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m)
    (eta := 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) heta

/-- A rational threshold can be chosen strictly between the true real
agreement scale and twice that scale. Thus the actual failed-zoom PR error
`2*e` is at most four times the real scale. -/
theorem selected_rational_failure_threshold_exists {m h : Nat} :
    exists e : Rat,
      manuscriptAgreementFloor m h < (e : Real) /\
      (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h := by
  have hpos : 0 < manuscriptAgreementFloor m h := by
    unfold manuscriptAgreementFloor
    exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _
  obtain ⟨e, helo, hehi⟩ :=
    exists_rat_btwn (show manuscriptAgreementFloor m h <
      2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h by nlinarith)
  exact ⟨e, helo, hehi⟩

theorem selected_rational_failure_eta_le_four_floor {m h : Nat}
    {e : Rat} (he : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) :
    2 * (e : Real) < 4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h := by
  linarith

/-- The finite HC coefficient bound instantiated with the real agreement
floor tolerates the rational rounding loss exactly as a factor four in eta. -/
theorem selected_rational_low_HC_sum_bound {h m : Nat} (hm : 0 < m)
    {e : Rat} (he0 : 0 <= e)
    (he : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) :
    selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := 2 * h) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
      (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) <=
      ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
          (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
            (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) := by
  have heta0 : 0 <= 2 * (e : Real) := by
    have he0Real : (0 : Real) <= (e : Real) := by exact_mod_cast he0
    exact mul_nonneg (by norm_num) he0Real
  have hTpos : 0 < sourceMomentT m := by
    unfold sourceMomentT
    exact Nat.pow_pos (by omega)
  have hExp : 1 <= m * sourceMomentT m := by
    exact Nat.one_le_iff_ne_zero.mpr
      (Nat.mul_ne_zero (Nat.ne_of_gt hm) (Nat.ne_of_gt hTpos))
  have hP2 : 2 <= sourceMomentP m := by
    unfold sourceMomentP
    simpa only [pow_one] using
      (Nat.pow_le_pow_right (n := 2) (i := 1) (by decide) hExp)
  have halpha : 0 <= ((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) /
      (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    apply div_nonneg
    · exact sub_nonneg.mpr (by exact_mod_cast hP2)
    · positivity
  have heta : 2 * (e : Real) <= 4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h := by
    have hround := selected_rational_failure_eta_le_four_floor he
    linarith
  have hpower := Real.rpow_le_rpow heta0 heta halpha
  have hsum := selected_low_HC46_constant_sum_bound
    (d := 2 * h) (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m)
    (eta := 2 * (e : Real)) heta0
  have hcoef0 : 0 <= ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
      (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) := by
    positivity
  calc
    selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := 2 * h) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
        (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) <=
      ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
          (2 * (e : Real)) ^
            (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) := by
              simpa using hsum
    _ <= ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
          (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
            (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) :=
      mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hpower hcoef0

/-- The actual rational failed-zoom HC sum is bounded by the dyadic
coefficient envelope used in the same-f exponent identity. -/
theorem selected_actual_low_HC_dyadic_envelope {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h)
    {e : Rat} (he0 : 0 <= e)
    (he : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) :
    selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := 2 * h) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
      (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) <=
        (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m)) := by
  have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
  have hcountNat := low_level_count_le_sqrt_dyadic h
  have hcountCast : ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) <=
      (2 : Real) ^ ((2 * (Nat.sqrt h + 1) : Nat) : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hcountNat
  have hcountRate := low_level_count_exponent_le_height_rate hmpos hfloor
  have hcountRateCast : ((2 * (Nat.sqrt h + 1) : Nat) : Real) <=
      (h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) := by
    push_cast
    exact hcountRate
  have hcount : ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) <=
      (2 : Real) ^ ((h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2)) := by
    exact hcountCast.trans (Real.rpow_le_rpow_of_exponent_le
      (by norm_num : (1 : Real) <= 2) hcountRateCast)
  have hfour : 4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h =
      (2 : Real) ^ (2 - 2 * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) * (h : Real)) := by
    unfold manuscriptAgreementFloor
    rw [show (4 : Real) = (2 : Real) ^ (2 : Real) by norm_num]
    rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2)]
    congr 1
    ring
  have hPposNat : 0 < sourceMomentP m := by
    unfold sourceMomentP
    exact Nat.pow_pos (by omega)
  have hPpos : (0 : Real) < (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hPposNat
  have hFloorPowerPos :
      0 < (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
        (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) :=
    Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by
      unfold manuscriptAgreementFloor
      positivity) _
  have hAlphaP :
    ((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) =
        1 - 2 / (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    field_simp [ne_of_gt hPpos]
  have hNatPowCast :
      ((500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m : Nat) : Real) =
        500 * (sourceRankExponent m : Real) ^ 2 * (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
    push_cast
    ring
  have hcoeffEq :
      (2 : Real) ^ ((h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2)) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
        (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
          (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) =
      (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m)) := by
    let a : Real := (h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2)
    let b : Real := 500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m
    let d : Real := 2 - 2 * (1 - 1000 * (fixedRho m : Real) ^ 2) * (h : Real)
    let alpha : Real :=
      ((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)
    have hbase : (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^ alpha =
        (2 : Real) ^ (d * alpha) := by
      dsimp [alpha, d]
      rw [hfour, Real.rpow_mul (by norm_num : (0 : Real) <= 2)]
    calc
      _ = ((2 : Real) ^ a * (2 : Real) ^ b) * (2 : Real) ^ (d * alpha) := by
        dsimp [a, b, alpha]
        rw [hbase]
        rw [← Real.rpow_natCast (2 : Real)
          (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m), hNatPowCast]
      _ = (2 : Real) ^ (a + b) * (2 : Real) ^ (d * alpha) := by
        rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2)]
      _ = (2 : Real) ^ (a + b + d * alpha) := by
        rw [← Real.rpow_add (by norm_num : (0 : Real) < 2)]
      _ = (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m)) := by
        congr 1
        dsimp [a, b, d, alpha]
        unfold sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent
        push_cast
        rw [hAlphaP]
        ring_nf
  have hhc := selected_rational_low_HC_sum_bound hmpos he0 he
  calc
    selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := 2 * h) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
        (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) <=
      ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
        (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
          (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) := hhc
    _ <= (2 : Real) ^ ((h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2)) *
        (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
        (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
          (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) := by
            have hbNonneg : 0 <= (2 : Real) ^
                (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) := by positivity
            calc
              _ = ((2 * h + 1 : Nat) : Real) *
                    (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m) *
                    (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
                      (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) := by ring
              _ <= ((2 : Real) ^ ((h : Real) / (1000 * (m : Real) ^ 2)) *
                    (2 : Real) ^ (500 * sourceRankExponent m ^ 2 * sourceMomentP m)) *
                    (4 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h) ^
                      (((sourceMomentP m : Real) - 2) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) := by
                exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right
                  (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcount hbNonneg)
                  hFloorPowerPos.le
              _ = _ := by ring
    _ = (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m)) := hcoeffEq

theorem source_high_error_exponent_gap_positive {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hh : 20001 * m ^ 2 <= h) :
    0 < sourceHighErrorExponentGap m h := by
  have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
  have hmR : (0 : Real) < (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hmpos
  have hmRlarge : (256 : Real) <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hm
  have hhR : 20001 * (m : Real) ^ 2 <= (h : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hh
  have hrho := fixed_rho_explicit hmpos
  have hrhoPos : 0 < (fixedRho m : Real) := by rw [hrho]; positivity
  have hcoeff :
      2 * (m : Real) <=
        (8 / 3 : Real) * (m : Real) - 2 +
          (3996 * (m : Real) + 2) * (fixedRho m : Real) := by
    have hbase : 2 * (m : Real) <= (8 / 3 : Real) * (m : Real) - 2 := by
      nlinarith
    have hprod : 0 <= (3996 * (m : Real) + 2) * (fixedRho m : Real) :=
      mul_nonneg (by positivity) hrhoPos.le
    exact hbase.trans (le_add_of_nonneg_right hprod)
  have hrNat : (sourceRankExponent m : Real) = 40000 * (m : Real) ^ 3 := by
    norm_num [sourceRankExponent, pow_succ]
  unfold sourceHighErrorExponentGap
  rw [hrNat]
  have hmul : 2 * (m : Real) * (20001 * (m : Real) ^ 2) =
      40002 * (m : Real) ^ 3 := by ring
  have hmSq : (1 : Real) <= (m : Real) ^ 2 := by nlinarith [hmRlarge]
  have hmCubeLower : (256 : Real) <= (m : Real) ^ 3 := by
    calc
      (256 : Real) = 256 * 1 := by ring
      _ <= (m : Real) * (m : Real) ^ 2 :=
        mul_le_mul hmRlarge hmSq (by norm_num) (by positivity)
      _ = (m : Real) ^ 3 := by ring
  have hmain :
      40002 * (m : Real) ^ 3 > 40000 * (m : Real) ^ 3 + 7 := by
    nlinarith [hmCubeLower]
  have hmulcoeff := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hcoeff
    (by positivity : (0 : Real) <= (h : Real))
  have hmulhh : 2 * (m : Real) * (20001 * (m : Real) ^ 2) <=
      2 * (m : Real) * (h : Real) :=
    mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hhR (by positivity)
  nlinarith [hmulhh, hmul, hmulcoeff, hmain]

def sourceSuccessScale (m h : Nat) : Real :=
  localManuscriptSuccessScale (localManuscriptRho m) m h

/-- The analytic margin scale is the manuscript's displayed scale with the
same fixed-rho parameter. This is an exponent identity, not a threshold
assumption. -/
theorem source_success_scale_eq_manuscript {m h : Nat} :
    sourceSuccessScale m h =
      localManuscriptSuccessScale (localManuscriptRho m) m h := by
  rfl

/-- Divisibility in the selected height identifies the source scale with the
existing rational success margin at precisely `badExponent m h`. -/
theorem source_success_scale_eq_successMargin {m h : Nat}
    (hm : 0 < m) (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h) :
    sourceSuccessScale m h =
      (ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.successMargin
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) : Real) := by
  rw [source_success_scale_eq_manuscript]
  exact local_manuscript_scale_eq_successMargin hm hdiv

/-- A lower bound on the same selected beta controls the negative Holder
power. This retains the beta-relative comparison required by the weighted
star signal; it does not replace that signal by an absolute success margin. -/
theorem selected_beta_negative_holder_power_le_signal
    {beta signal q : Real} (hsignal : 0 < signal)
    (hbeta : signal <= beta) (hq : 0 <= q) :
    beta ^ (-q) <= signal ^ (-q) := by
  have hbetaPos : 0 < beta := lt_of_lt_of_le hsignal hbeta
  have hpow := Real.rpow_le_rpow (le_of_lt hsignal) hbeta hq
  have hsignalPow : 0 < signal ^ q := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hsignal q
  have hbetaPow : 0 < beta ^ q := Real.rpow_pos_of_pos hbetaPos q
  rw [Real.rpow_neg (le_of_lt hbetaPos), Real.rpow_neg (le_of_lt hsignal)]
  exact (inv_le_inv₀ hbetaPow hsignalPow).mpr hpow

theorem source_successMargin_pos (E : Nat) : 0 < successMargin E := by
  unfold ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.successMargin
  exact div_pos (by norm_num) (pow_pos (by norm_num) E)

theorem selected_matching_center_mass_nonneg
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [Fintype V]
    {t d m : Nat} (htd : t <= d)
    (hdV : d <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V)
    (C : CenterTable (V := V) t) (f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) V) :
    0 <= matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f := by
  unfold matchingCenterMass
  exact ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.eventMass_nonneg _ _

def sourceAgreementScale (m h : Nat) : Real := manuscriptAgreementFloor m h

def sourceThresholdScale (m h : Nat) : Real := manuscriptMomentThreshold m h

/-- The accepted global-functional selection gives a genuine additive
selected-f signal. Combined with `X_f <= beta_f`, its positive unconditional
term proves the selected beta lower bound; beta is not supplied as a margin
hypothesis. -/
theorem ordinary_selected_functional_beta_lower
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [Fintype V]
    {t d m E : Nat}
    (htd : t <= d) (hdV : d <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V)
    (hk : 1 <= d - t)
    (hguard : m * (d - t) + E + 2 <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t)
    (hcenter : Nonempty (Grass V t))
    (hleaf : forall K : Grass V t, Nonempty (LeafOver K d))
    (C : CenterTable (V := V) t) (T : LeafTable (V := V) d)
    (hscore : successMargin E <= StarDensity (k := m) hcenter hleaf C T) :
    exists f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) V,
      let beta := matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f
      let M : Rat := 2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V -
        (t + m * (d - t)))
      let F : Rat := 2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V
      (successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) <= beta := by
  obtain ⟨f, hselected⟩ :=
    ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.ordinary_star_selects_weighted_functional
      htd hdV hk hguard hcenter hleaf C T hscore
  refine ⟨f, ?_⟩
  dsimp at hselected ⊢
  rcases hselected with ⟨hsignal, hdom⟩
  have hfirst : 0 <=
      (successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t)) *
        matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f := by
    have hc0 : 0 <= successMargin E / 4 :=
      div_nonneg (source_successMargin_pos E).le (by norm_num)
    have hratio0 : 0 <=
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t) : Rat) :=
      div_nonneg (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _) (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _)
    exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hc0 hratio0)
      (selected_matching_center_mass_nonneg htd hdV C f)
  have hcombine := hsignal.trans hdom
  nlinarith

/-- The same ordinary weighted-star witness carries the exact beta floor
needed for the negative Holder power in the selected analytic estimate. -/
theorem ordinary_selected_beta_controls_holder_loss
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [Fintype V]
    {t d m E P : Nat}
    (htd : t <= d) (hdV : d <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V)
    (hk : 1 <= d - t)
    (hguard : m * (d - t) + E + 2 <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t)
    (hcenter : Nonempty (Grass V t))
    (hleaf : forall K : Grass V t, Nonempty (LeafOver K d))
    (C : CenterTable (V := V) t) (T : LeafTable (V := V) d)
    (hscore : successMargin E <= StarDensity (k := m) hcenter hleaf C T)
    (hP : 0 < P) :
    exists f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) V,
      let beta : Real :=
        (matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f : Real)
      let signal : Real :=
        (((successMargin E / 4) *
          (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
            2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) : Rat) : Real)
      beta ^ (-(m : Real) / (P : Real)) <=
        signal ^ (-(m : Real) / (P : Real)) := by
  obtain ⟨f, hbeta⟩ := ordinary_selected_functional_beta_lower
    htd hdV hk hguard hcenter hleaf C T hscore
  refine ⟨f, ?_⟩
  dsimp
  dsimp at hbeta
  have hsignal : 0 <
      (((successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) : Rat) : Real) := by
    have hsignalRat : 0 < (successMargin E / 4 : Rat) *
        ((2 : Rat) ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          (2 : Rat) ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) := by
      exact mul_pos (div_pos (source_successMargin_pos E) (by norm_num))
        (div_pos (pow_pos (by norm_num) _) (pow_pos (by norm_num) _))
    exact_mod_cast hsignalRat
  have hbetaReal :
      (((successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) : Rat) : Real) <=
        (matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hbeta
  have hholder := selected_beta_negative_holder_power_le_signal hsignal hbetaReal
    (show 0 <= (m : Real) / (P : Real) by positivity)
  simpa [neg_div] using hholder

/-- Full same-functional output of weighted selection together with the beta
Holder-loss estimate. The selected star mass bound, domination by beta, and
unconditional beta floor all belong to one witness `f`. -/
theorem ordinary_selected_same_witness_signal_and_holder_loss
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [Fintype V]
    {t d m E P : Nat}
    (htd : t <= d) (hdV : d <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V)
    (hk : 1 <= d - t)
    (hguard : m * (d - t) + E + 2 <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t)
    (hcenter : Nonempty (Grass V t))
    (hleaf : forall K : Grass V t, Nonempty (LeafOver K d))
    (C : CenterTable (V := V) t) (T : LeafTable (V := V) d)
    (hscore : successMargin E <= StarDensity (k := m) hcenter hleaf C T)
    (hP : 0 < P) :
    exists f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) V,
      let betaRat := matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f
      let X := matchingStarMass (m := m) htd hdV C T f
      let M : Rat := 2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V -
        (t + m * (d - t)))
      let B : Rat := 2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t)
      let F : Rat := 2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V
      let beta : Real := (betaRat : Real)
      let signal : Real := (((successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) : Rat) : Real)
      (successMargin E / 4) * (M / B) * betaRat +
          (successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) <= X /\
        X <= betaRat /\
        (successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) <= betaRat /\
        beta ^ (-(m : Real) / (P : Real)) <=
          signal ^ (-(m : Real) / (P : Real)) := by
  obtain ⟨f, hselected⟩ :=
    ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.ordinary_star_selects_weighted_functional
      htd hdV hk hguard hcenter hleaf C T hscore
  dsimp at hselected
  rcases hselected with ⟨hsignal, hdom⟩
