  have hbetaFloor :
      (successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) <=
        matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f := by
    have hterm0 : 0 <=
        (successMargin E / 4) *
          (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
            2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t)) *
          matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f := by
      have hc0 : 0 <= successMargin E / 4 :=
        div_nonneg (source_successMargin_pos E).le (by norm_num)
      have hratio0 : 0 <=
          (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
            2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - t) : Rat) :=
        div_nonneg (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _) (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _)
      exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hc0 hratio0)
        (selected_matching_center_mass_nonneg htd hdV C f)
    have hcombine := hsignal.trans hdom
    nlinarith
  have hsignalPos : 0 <
      (((successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) : Rat) : Real) := by
    have hsignalRat : 0 < (successMargin E / 4 : Rat) *
        ((2 : Rat) ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          (2 : Rat) ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) := by
      exact mul_pos (div_pos (source_successMargin_pos E) (by norm_num))
        (div_pos (pow_pos (by norm_num) _) (pow_pos (by norm_num) _))
    exact_mod_cast hsignalRat
  have hbetaReal :
      (((successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (t + m * (d - t))) /
          2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V) : Rat) : Real) <=
        (matchingCenterMass (m := m) htd hdV C f : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hbetaFloor
  refine ⟨f, ?_⟩
  dsimp
  refine ⟨hsignal, hdom, hbetaFloor, ?_⟩
  have hholder := selected_beta_negative_holder_power_le_signal hsignalPos hbetaReal
    (show 0 <= (m : Real) / (P : Real) by positivity)
  simpa [neg_div] using hholder

set_option pp.all true in
/-- The actual side-law score, after complement selection and exact coordinate
transport, chooses one coordinate functional carrying the full weighted-star
signal and its beta-floor/Holder consequence.  This is the single witness
that can be passed unchanged to the finite moment and inverse consumers. -/
theorem actual_side_score_selects_same_coordinate_functional
    {N rows : Nat} (I : ActualOccurrenceAllocation.Instance N rows)
    (copies : Nat) {J : Nat} (U : TaggedGoodU I copies J)
    {t h k E P : Nat} (ht : t ≤ 2 * h) (hh : h ≤ J)
    (C : ActualTaggedFixedTableAcceptance.TaggedCenterTable I copies)
    (T : ActualTaggedFixedTableAcceptance.TaggedLeafTable I copies)
    (hk : 1 ≤ 2 * h - t)
    (hguard : k * (2 * h - t) + E + 2 ≤ 2 * J - t)
    (hscore : ActualTaggedMZSideDraw.sideConditionalDensity
        I copies U t h k C T ≥ successMargin E)
    (hP : 0 < P) :
    ∃ A : SideComplement I copies U,
    ∃ f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J),
      let Cc := selectedCoordinateCenterTable (t := t) I copies U A C
      let Tc : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable (V := CoordAmbient J)
          (t + (2 * h - t)) := selectedCoordinateLeafTable I copies U A T
      let betaRat := matchingCenterMass (V := CoordAmbient J) (m := k)
        (Nat.le_add_right t (2 * h - t))
        (by
          have hs : t + (2 * h - t) = 2 * h := Nat.add_sub_of_le ht
          rw [hs]
          simpa [CoordAmbient] using (show 2 * h ≤ 2 * J by omega)) Cc f
      let X := matchingStarMass (V := CoordAmbient J) (m := k)
        (Nat.le_add_right t (2 * h - t))
        (by
          have hs : t + (2 * h - t) = 2 * h := Nat.add_sub_of_le ht
          rw [hs]
          simpa [CoordAmbient] using (show 2 * h ≤ 2 * J by omega)) Cc Tc f
      let M : Rat := 2 ^ (2 * J - (t + k * (2 * h - t)))
      let B : Rat := 2 ^ (2 * J - t)
      let F : Rat := 2 ^ (2 * J)
      let beta : Real := (betaRat : Real)
      let signal : Real := (((successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) : Rat) : Real)
      (successMargin E / 4) * (M / B) * betaRat +
          (successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) ≤ X ∧
        X ≤ betaRat ∧
        (successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) ≤ betaRat ∧
        beta ^ (-(k : Real) / (P : Real)) ≤
          signal ^ (-(k : Real) / (P : Real)) ∧
        successMargin E ≤ StarDensity (V := CoordAmbient J) (k := k)
          (grass_nonempty_of_le (by
            calc
              t ≤ 2 * h := ht
              _ ≤ 2 * J := by omega
              _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J) := by
                simp [CoordAmbient]))
           (fun K => extension_nonempty K
             (show t ≤ t + (2 * h - t) by omega)
             (by
               have hs : t + (2 * h - t) = 2 * h := Nat.add_sub_of_le ht
               rw [hs]
               simpa [CoordAmbient] using (show 2 * h ≤ 2 * J by omega))) Cc Tc := by
  have hscoreA := actual_side_score_selects_coordinate_complement
    I copies U ht hh C T hscore
  obtain ⟨A, hscoreCoord⟩ := hscoreA
  have hdCoord : 2 * h ≤ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J) := by
    simpa [CoordAmbient] using (show 2 * h ≤ 2 * J by omega)
  have hsplitCoord : t + (2 * h - t) = 2 * h := Nat.add_sub_of_le ht
  have hdCoordSrc : t + (2 * h - t) <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J) := by
    rw [hsplitCoord]
    exact hdCoord
  have hkSrc : 1 <= (t + (2 * h - t)) - t := by
    rw [hsplitCoord]
    exact hk
  have hguardCoord : k * (2 * h - t) + E + 2 <=
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J) - t := by
    simpa [CoordAmbient] using hguard
  have hguardCoordSrc : k * ((t + (2 * h - t)) - t) + E + 2 <=
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J) - t := by
    rw [hsplitCoord]
    exact hguardCoord
  have hcenter : Nonempty (Grass (CoordAmbient J) t) :=
    grass_nonempty_of_le (by
      calc
        t <= 2 * h := ht
        _ <= 2 * J := by omega
        _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J) := by
          simp [CoordAmbient])
  have hleaf : forall K : Grass (CoordAmbient J) t,
      Nonempty (LeafOver K (t + (2 * h - t))) :=
    fun K => extension_nonempty K
      (show t <= t + (2 * h - t) by omega)
      (by
        have hs : t + (2 * h - t) = 2 * h := Nat.add_sub_of_le ht
        rw [hs]
        simpa [CoordAmbient] using (show 2 * h <= 2 * J by omega))
  let Cc := selectedCoordinateCenterTable (t := t) I copies U A C
  let Tc : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable (V := CoordAmbient J) (t + (2 * h - t)) :=
    selectedCoordinateLeafTable I copies U A T
  let ScoreIndex := {d : Nat // t <= d /\
    d <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (CoordAmbient J)}
  let scoreAt : ScoreIndex -> Prop := fun i =>
    successMargin E <= StarDensity (V := CoordAmbient J) (k := k)
      (grass_nonempty_of_le (i.property.1.trans i.property.2))
      (fun K => extension_nonempty K i.property.1 i.property.2)
      Cc (selectedCoordinateLeafTable (d := i.val) I copies U A T)
  let scoreTarget : ScoreIndex :=
    ⟨t + (2 * h - t), Nat.le_add_right t (2 * h - t), hdCoordSrc⟩
  let scoreWide : ScoreIndex := ⟨2 * h, ht, hdCoord⟩
  have hscoreIndex : scoreTarget = scoreWide := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    dsimp [scoreTarget, scoreWide]
    exact hsplitCoord
  have hscoreCoord' : successMargin E <=
      StarDensity (V := CoordAmbient J) (k := k) hcenter hleaf Cc Tc := by
    have hscoreWide : scoreAt scoreWide := by
      simpa [scoreAt, scoreWide, Cc, Tc] using hscoreCoord
    have hscoreTarget : scoreAt scoreTarget :=
      (congrArg scoreAt hscoreIndex).symm ▸ hscoreWide
    simpa [scoreAt, scoreTarget, Cc, Tc] using hscoreTarget
  have hsel := ordinary_selected_same_witness_signal_and_holder_loss
    (V := CoordAmbient J) (t := t) (d := t + (2 * h - t)) (m := k) (E := E) (P := P)
    (Nat.le_add_right t (2 * h - t))
    (by rw [hsplitCoord]; exact hdCoord) hkSrc hguardCoordSrc
    hcenter hleaf Cc Tc hscoreCoord' hP
  rcases hsel with ⟨f, hf⟩
  refine ⟨A, ?_⟩
  dsimp only
  refine ⟨f, ?_⟩
  simpa [Cc, Tc, CoordAmbient, hsplitCoord, and_assoc] using
    (And.intro hf hscoreCoord')

/-- The actual coordinate selected leaf, exact rational failed-zoom error,
and the weighted selector's single functional produce the low-HC part of the
analytic comparison against that same functional's first weighted-star
signal. The selected beta floor is derived from the selector's additive
signal; it is not a caller-supplied beta hypothesis. -/
theorem source_mass_dimension_of_guard {n c s m E : Nat}
    (hm : 1 ≤ m)
    (hguard : m * s + E + 2 ≤
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) - c) :
    c + s ≤ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) := by
  have hn : Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) = n := by simp
  have hguardN : m * s + E + 2 ≤ n - c := by
    simpa only [hn] using hguard
  have hfull : c + m * s ≤ n := by omega
  have hsMul : s ≤ m * s := Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left s (by omega)
  have hnatDim : c + s ≤ n := by omega
  simpa only [hn] using hnatDim

theorem selected_actual_same_witness_low_HC_signal_bound
    {n c s h m E : Nat} {rho : Real} {e : Rat}
    (C : ActualSourceStarLaw.CenterTable
      (V := Fin n → ZMod 2) c)
    (T : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable
      (V := Fin n → ZMod 2) (c + s))
    (hm : 256 ≤ m) (hmpos : 0 < m)
    (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m ≤ h)
    (hEven : c + s = 2 * h)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h)
    (hE : E = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
    (hrd : sourceRankExponent m < c + s)
    (he0 : 0 ≤ e)
    (he : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h)
    (hfail : ∀ (q : Nat) (Q : Grass (Fin n → ZMod 2) q)
      (P : ActualMaximalPairLadder.DecodedPair Q (c + s)),
      q + ActualMaximalPairLadder.codim P.W = sourceRankExponent m →
        Fintype.card (ActualMaximalPairLadder.Zoom Q P) ≠ 0 →
          ActualMaximalPairLadder.agreement (fun L => T L) Q P ≤ e)
    (hc : (c : Real) = 2 * (1 - rho) * (h : Real))
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * rho * (h : Real))
    (hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = rho)
    (hcenter : Nonempty (Grass (Fin n → ZMod 2) c))
    (hleaf : ∀ K : Grass (Fin n → ZMod 2) c,
      Nonempty (LeafOver K (c + s)))
    (hk : 1 ≤ s)
    (hguard : m * s + E + 2 ≤
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) - c)
    (hscore : successMargin E ≤
      StarDensity (k := m) hcenter hleaf C T)
    (hHC : HC46ExactContract) :
    ∃ f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2),
      let beta : Real := (matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s)
        (source_mass_dimension_of_guard (by omega) hguard) C f : Real)
      let X := matchingStarMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s)
        (source_mass_dimension_of_guard (by omega) hguard) C T f
      (successMargin E / 4) *
          (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) - (c + m * s)) /
            2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) - c)) *
          matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s)
            (source_mass_dimension_of_guard (by omega) hguard) C f +
      (successMargin E / 4) *
          (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) - (c + m * s)) /
            2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2)) ≤ X ∧
      lpNorm (sourceMomentP m) (fun M : BinaryMatrix n c =>
        appendAverage (selectedLow (selectedF T f)
          (r := sourceRankExponent m)) M) ≤
        selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s)
          (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) ∧
      2 ^ (m + 1) * beta ^
          (1 - (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
          (selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s)
            (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m)
            (2 * (e : Real))) ^ m ≤
        (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) * beta := by
  have hdim : c + s ≤ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) := by
    exact source_mass_dimension_of_guard (by omega) hguard
  have hsub : (c + s) - c = s := Nat.add_sub_cancel_left c s
  have hk' : 1 ≤ (c + s) - c := by simpa [hsub] using hk
  have hguard' : m * ((c + s) - c) + E + 2 ≤
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) - c := by
    rw [hsub]
    exact hguard
  have hsel := ordinary_star_selects_weighted_functional
    (m := m) (E := E) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim hk' hguard'
    hcenter hleaf C T hscore
  obtain ⟨f, hselected⟩ := hsel
  dsimp at hselected
  rcases hselected with ⟨hweighted, hdom⟩
  simp only [hsub] at hweighted hdom
  have hcoef0 : 0 ≤
      (successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) -
          (c + m * s)) /
          2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) - c)) *
        matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim C f := by
    have hsm : 0 <= successMargin E / 4 :=
      div_nonneg (source_successMargin_pos E).le (by norm_num)
    have hratio : 0 <=
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n -> ZMod 2) -
          (c + m * s)) /
          2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n -> ZMod 2) - c) : Rat) :=
      div_nonneg (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _) (pow_nonneg (by norm_num) _)
    exact mul_nonneg (mul_nonneg hsm hratio)
      (selected_matching_center_mass_nonneg
        (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim C f)
  have hbetaRat :
      (successMargin E / 4) *
        (2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) -
          (c + m * s)) /
          2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2)) ≤
        matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim C f := by
    have hcombine := hweighted.trans hdom
    nlinarith
  have hbetaReal :
      ((((successMargin E / 4) *
        ((2 : Rat) ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) -
          (c + m * s)) /
          (2 : Rat) ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2))) : Rat) : Real) ≤
        (matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim C f : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast hbetaRat
  have hdimMoment : c + m * s ≤ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2) := by
    omega
  have hbetaFloor := selected_beta_signal_eq_source_floor
    hmpos hdiv hE hdimMoment hrho hc hs
  have hbetaFloorReal :
      (2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h ≤
        (matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim C f : Real) := by
    rw [← hbetaFloor]
    exact hbetaReal
  have hPR := ActualLeafLabelRankImageAlignment.actual_leaf_failed_zoom_gives_nominal_pseudorandom
    hrd T f e he0 hfail
  have hP4 : 4 ≤ sourceMomentP m := by
    have hT : 0 < sourceMomentT m := by
      unfold sourceMomentT
      exact Nat.pow_pos (by omega)
    have hm2 : 2 ≤ m := by omega
    have hT1 : 1 ≤ sourceMomentT m := Nat.succ_le_of_lt hT
    have hExp : 2 ≤ m * sourceMomentT m := by
      calc
        2 = 2 * 1 := by norm_num
        _ ≤ m * sourceMomentT m := Nat.mul_le_mul hm2 hT1
    unfold sourceMomentP
    calc
      4 = 2 ^ 2 := by norm_num
      _ ≤ 2 ^ (m * sourceMomentT m) := Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by decide) hExp
  have hPpos : 0 < (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by positivity
  have hPdyadic : ∃ q : Nat, sourceMomentP m = 2 ^ q := by
    exact ⟨m * sourceMomentT m, rfl⟩
  have hlowNorm := selected_low_append_HC46_lpNorm_bound
    (b := selectedF T f) (p := sourceMomentP m) hEven hPR hP4 hPdyadic hHC
  have hlowEnvelope := selected_actual_low_HC_dyadic_envelope
    hm hfloor he0 he
  have hlowEnvelope' : selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s)
      (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) <=
        (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m)) := by
    simpa [hEven] using hlowEnvelope
  have hlow0 : 0 ≤ selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s)
      (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) := by
    unfold selectedLowHC46NormBound
    exact Finset.sum_nonneg (fun i hi => by positivity)
  have hlowSignal := selected_low_analytic_term_le_same_functional_signal
    hm hfloor (by unfold sourceMomentP; positivity) rfl
    hbetaFloorReal hlow0 hlowEnvelope'
  refine ⟨f, ?_⟩
  dsimp only
  exact ⟨hweighted, hlowNorm, hlowSignal⟩

/-- The low-level Holder and HC46 contribution for a fixed, already selected
functional.  Its PR input is derived directly from that functional's complete
failed-zoom family, so this lemma cannot select a replacement witness. -/
theorem selected_fixed_functional_low_HC_signal_bound
    {n c s h m : Nat} {e : Rat} {beta : Real}
    (T : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable (V := Fin n → ZMod 2) (c + s))
    (f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) (Fin n → ZMod 2))
    (hm : 256 ≤ m) (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m ≤ h)
    (hEven : c + s = 2 * h)
    (hrd : sourceRankExponent m < c + s)
    (he0 : 0 ≤ e) (he : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h)
    (hfail : ∀ (q : Nat) (Q : Grass (Fin n → ZMod 2) q)
      (P : ActualMaximalPairLadder.DecodedPair Q (c + s)),
      q + ActualMaximalPairLadder.codim P.W = sourceRankExponent m →
        Fintype.card (ActualMaximalPairLadder.Zoom Q P) ≠ 0 →
          ActualMaximalPairLadder.agreement (fun L => T L) Q P ≤ e)
    (hbetaFloor : (2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h ≤ beta)
    (hHC : ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticMoment.HC46ExactContract) :
    lpNorm (sourceMomentP m)
        (fun M : BinaryMatrix n c =>
          appendAverage (selectedLow (selectedF T f)
            (r := sourceRankExponent m)) M) <=
      selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
        (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) ∧
    (2 : Real) ^ (m + 1) * beta ^
        (1 - (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
        (selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
          (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real))) ^ m <=
      (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) * beta := by
  have hPR :=
    ActualLeafLabelRankImageAlignment.actual_leaf_failed_zoom_gives_nominal_pseudorandom
      hrd T f e he0 hfail
  have hP4 : 4 ≤ sourceMomentP m := by
    have hT : 0 < sourceMomentT m := by
      unfold sourceMomentT
      exact Nat.pow_pos (by omega)
    have hm2 : 2 ≤ m := by omega
    have hT1 : 1 ≤ sourceMomentT m := Nat.succ_le_of_lt hT
    have hExp : 2 ≤ m * sourceMomentT m := by
      calc
        2 = 2 * 1 := by norm_num
        _ ≤ m * sourceMomentT m := Nat.mul_le_mul hm2 hT1
    unfold sourceMomentP
    calc
      4 = 2 ^ 2 := by norm_num
      _ ≤ 2 ^ (m * sourceMomentT m) := Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by decide) hExp
  have hPdyadic : ∃ q : Nat, sourceMomentP m = 2 ^ q :=
    ⟨m * sourceMomentT m, rfl⟩
  have hlowNorm := selected_low_append_HC46_lpNorm_bound
    (b := selectedF T f) (p := sourceMomentP m) hEven hPR hP4 hPdyadic hHC
  have hlowEnvelope := selected_actual_low_HC_dyadic_envelope
    hm hfloor he0 he
  have hlowEnvelope' : selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s)
      (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) <=
        (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowCoefficientEnvelopeExponent m h (sourceMomentP m)) := by
    simpa [hEven] using hlowEnvelope
  have hlow0 : 0 ≤ selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s)
      (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real)) := by
    unfold selectedLowHC46NormBound
    exact Finset.sum_nonneg (fun i hi => by positivity)
  have hlowSignal := selected_low_analytic_term_le_same_functional_signal
    hm hfloor (by unfold sourceMomentP; positivity) rfl
    hbetaFloor hlow0 hlowEnvelope'
  exact ⟨hlowNorm, hlowSignal⟩

/-- Parseval aggregated over every integral rank level.  This is the finite
identity needed to control the total selected high-level energy without an
extra factor for the number of levels. -/
theorem rankProjection_all_levels_energy_eq {n d : Nat}
    (F : BinaryMatrix n d -> Real) :
    (Finset.sum (Finset.range (d + 1)) (fun i =>
      uniformMean (fun M : BinaryMatrix n d =>
        (rankProjection i F M) ^ 2))) = uniformMean (fun M => F M ^ 2) :=
  ActualSelectedHighEnergyTail.rankProjection_all_levels_energy_eq F

/-- Boolean indicators have squared matrix mean at most one. -/
theorem indicator_squared_mean_le_one {n d : Nat} (b : BinaryMatrix n d -> Bool) :
    uniformMean (fun M => (indicator b M) ^ 2) <= 1 :=
  ActualSelectedHighEnergyTail.indicator_squared_mean_le_one b

/-- Each spectral rank layer of the actual Boolean indicator carries at most
unit squared energy, by the proved Fourier Parseval/Bessel identity. -/
theorem selected_rank_layer_energy_le_one {n d i : Nat}
    (b : BinaryMatrix n d -> Bool) :
    uniformMean (fun M : BinaryMatrix n d =>
      (rankProjection i (indicator b) M) ^ 2) <= 1 :=
  ActualSelectedHighEnergyTail.selected_rank_layer_energy_le_one b

/-- A Parseval-controlled weighted level sum is at most its largest weight.
The high index set may be any subset of the available ranks; the proof uses
the full rank-energy identity above, not a per-level bound multiplied by d. -/
theorem selected_weighted_levels_le_max_weight {n d : Nat}
    (F : BinaryMatrix n d -> Real) (S : Finset (Fin (d + 1)))
    (weight : Fin (d + 1) -> Real) (B : Real)
    (hB : 0 <= B)
    (hweight0 : forall i, i ∈ S -> 0 <= weight i)
    (hweight : forall i, i ∈ S -> weight i <= B)
    (hFenergy : uniformMean (fun M => F M ^ 2) <= 1) :
    Finset.sum S (fun i => weight i *
      uniformMean (fun M : BinaryMatrix n d =>
        (rankProjection i.val F M) ^ 2)) <= B :=
  ActualSelectedHighEnergyTail.selected_weighted_levels_le_max_weight
    F S weight B hB hweight0 hweight hFenergy

/-- Pointwise spectral coefficient estimate under the explicit ambient
condition in Lemma 4.8. For i>r, the first spectral term contributes at most
half of 2^{-r(s-1)} and the ambient term contributes at most three eighths. -/
theorem selected_spectral_coefficient_le_tail {d i r s n : Nat}
    (hi : r < i) (hiD : i <= d)
    (hb : 1 <= (s : Real) - 1)
    (hn : (d : Real) - (n : Real) <=
      -(r : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1) - 3) :
    (2 : Real) ^ (-(i : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) +
        3 * (2 : Real) ^ ((i : Real) - (n : Real)) <=
      (2 : Real) ^ (-(r : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) :=
  ActualSelectedHighEnergyTail.selected_spectral_coefficient_le_tail
    hi hiD hb hn

/-- The actual selected Boolean leaf's high spectral expression is bounded by
the source tail after Parseval aggregates all high rank energies. Conditions
are stated in the source's integral dimensions; no assumed high-energy field
is introduced. -/
theorem selected_actual_high_spectral_sum_le_tail {n d r s : Nat}
    (b : BinaryMatrix n d -> Bool)
    (hb : 1 <= (s : Real) - 1)
    (hn : (d : Real) - (n : Real) <=
      -(r : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1) - 3) :
    Finset.sum (selectedHighFinIndexSet d r) (fun i =>
      ((2 : Real) ^ (-(i.val : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) +
        3 * (2 : Real) ^ ((i.val : Real) - (n : Real))) *
        uniformMean (fun W : BinaryMatrix n d =>
          (rankProjection i.val (indicator b) W) ^ 2)) <=
      (2 : Real) ^ (-(r : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) :=
  ActualSelectedHighEnergyTail.selected_actual_high_spectral_sum_le_tail
    b hb hn

/-- The explicit enlarged-height and block-growth guards imply the two
ambient hypotheses used in the high spectral tail estimate. The only
dimension growth used is the selected sampler's existing `h^2 < J`. -/
theorem source_tail_guards_of_growth {m h J s : Nat}
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hhS : 4000 * m ^ 2 <= h)
    (hh12 : 12 * m <= h) (hJ : h ^ 2 < J)
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * (fixedRho m : Real) * (h : Real)) :
    1 <= (s : Real) - 1 /\
    (2 * (h : Real) : Real) - (2 * (J : Real)) <=
      -(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1) - 3 := by
  have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
  have hmR : (0 : Real) < (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hmpos
  have hhS_R : 4000 * (m : Real) ^ 2 <= (h : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hhS
  have hh12_R : 12 * (m : Real) <= (h : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hh12
  have hJ_R : (h : Real) ^ 2 < (J : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hJ
  have hrho := fixed_rho_explicit hmpos
  have hsExact : (s : Real) = (h : Real) / (2000 * (m : Real) ^ 2) := by
    rw [hrho] at hs
    rw [hs]
    field_simp [ne_of_gt hmR]
    ring
  have hs2 : 2 <= (s : Real) := by
    rw [hsExact]
    rw [le_div_iff₀ (by positivity : (0 : Real) < 2000 * (m : Real) ^ 2)]
    nlinarith
  have hb : 1 <= (s : Real) - 1 := by linarith
  have hr : (sourceRankExponent m : Real) = 40000 * (m : Real) ^ 3 := by
    norm_num [sourceRankExponent, pow_succ]
  have hproduct :
      (sourceRankExponent m : Real) * (s : Real) <=
        20 * (m : Real) * (h : Real) := by
    rw [hr, hsExact]
    field_simp [ne_of_gt hmR]
    nlinarith
  have hprodsub :
      (sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1) <=
        20 * (m : Real) * (h : Real) := by
    have hrnonneg : 0 <= (sourceRankExponent m : Real) := by positivity
    have hsSub : (s : Real) - 1 <= (s : Real) := by linarith
    have hmul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsSub hrnonneg
    exact hmul.trans hproduct
  constructor
  · exact hb
  · have hJplus : h ^ 2 + 1 <= J := Nat.succ_le_of_lt hJ
    have hJplusR : (h : Real) ^ 2 + 1 <= (J : Real) := by
      exact_mod_cast hJplus
    have hlarge :
        2 * (h : Real) ^ 2 + 2 <= 2 * (J : Real) := by nlinarith [hJplusR]
    have hsq :
        12 * (m : Real) * (h : Real) <= (h : Real) ^ 2 :=
      (mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hh12_R (by positivity)).trans_eq (by rw [pow_two])
    have hgap : 20 * (m : Real) + 2 <= (h : Real) := by
      have hmRlarge : (256 : Real) <= (m : Real) := by exact_mod_cast hm
      nlinarith [hhS_R, hmRlarge]
    have hgapMul := mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_right hgap
      (show 0 <= (h : Real) by positivity)
    have hcompare :
          20 * (m : Real) * (h : Real) + 2 * (h : Real) + 3 <=
          2 * (h : Real) ^ 2 + 2 := by nlinarith [hsq, hgapMul]
    nlinarith

theorem source_tail_guards_of_margin_floor {m h J s : Nat}
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hfloor : sourceMarginHeightFloor m <= h)
    (hJ : h ^ 2 < J)
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * (fixedRho m : Real) * (h : Real)) :
    1 <= (s : Real) - 1 /\
    (2 * (h : Real) : Real) - (2 * (J : Real)) <=
      -(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1) - 3 := by
  have hmid : max (sourceTailHeightFloor m) (20001 * m ^ 2) <= h :=
    (Nat.le_max_left _ _).trans hfloor
  have htail : sourceTailHeightFloor m <= h :=
    (Nat.le_max_left _ _).trans hmid
  have hhS : 4000 * m ^ 2 <= h :=
    (Nat.le_max_left _ _).trans htail
  have hh12 : 12 * m <= h :=
    (Nat.le_max_right _ _).trans htail
  exact source_tail_guards_of_growth hm hhS hh12 hJ hs

/-- The margin-height source cutoff and selected sampler dimensions give the
exact spectral guards at the actual chosen height. The bound h^2 < J is
proved from blocks samplerA h and samplerA >= 1, not supplied as a tail
assumption. -/
theorem selected_actual_tail_guards
    (base : Nat -> Nat) (cutoff : Real -> Nat) {L m samplerA : Nat}
    (hsel : ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector
      (fun j => max (ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
        base cutoff j) (j + 2)) L =
        (m : WithBot Nat))
    (hsampler : 1 <= samplerA)
    (hmargin : sourceMarginHeightFloor m <= cutoff
      (1 / (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real))) :
    let h := ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m
    let J := SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA h
    let s := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h
    1 <= (s : Real) - 1 /\
      (2 * (h : Real) : Real) - (2 * (J : Real)) <=
        -(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1) - 3 := by
  dsimp
  let h := ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m
  let J := SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA h
  let s := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h
  have hparams := ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.selected_spectral_parameters
    base cutoff hsel
  rcases hparams with
    ⟨hm256, hh, hdiv, hhpos, hspos, hsplit, hrhoPos, hrho, hcReal,
      hsReal, hbaseFloor, hcutFloor⟩
  have hm : 0 < m := by omega
  have hrhoEq : ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.actualSelectedRho m h =
      ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho m := by
    calc
      ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.actualSelectedRho m h =
          1 / (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real) := hrho
      _ = ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho m := by
        rw [fixed_rho_explicit hm]
        norm_num [ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf]
  have hsFixed : (s : Real) =
      2 * (ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho m : Real) * (h : Real) := by
    dsimp [s]
    rw [hrhoEq] at hsReal
    exact hsReal
  have hcutFloorB : cutoff
      (1 / (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real)) <= h := by
    simpa [hrho] using hcutFloor
  have hheight : sourceMarginHeightFloor m <= h :=
    hmargin.trans hcutFloorB
  have hJ : h ^ 2 < J := by
    dsimp [J, h]
    exact selected_height_square_lt_blocks hsampler
  exact source_tail_guards_of_margin_floor hm256 hheight hJ hsFixed

/-- The selected height cutoff supplies the enlarged low-HC floor used in
the actual rational-threshold envelope. The floor is derived from the
selector's real-rho cutoff and the existing source margin cutoff. -/
theorem selected_actual_low_HC_floor
    (base : Nat → Nat) (cutoff : Real → Nat) {L m : Nat}
    (hsel : ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector
      (fun j => max (ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
        base cutoff j) (j + 2)) L = (m : WithBot Nat))
    (hmargin : sourceMarginHeightFloor m <=
      cutoff (1 / (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real))) :
    sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <=
      ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m := by
  have hparams := ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.selected_spectral_parameters
    base cutoff hsel
  rcases hparams with
    ⟨_, _, _, _, _, _, _, hrho, _, _, _, hcutFloor⟩
  have hcut : cutoff (1 / (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real)) <=
      ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m := by
    simpa [hrho] using hcutFloor
  have hheight : sourceMarginHeightFloor m <=
      ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m := hmargin.trans hcut
  exact (Nat.le_max_right _ _).trans hheight

/-- Concrete same-functional fields supplied by the ordinary weighted-star
selector: its weighted lower signal, atom-to-center domination, additive beta
floor, and negative Holder comparison. -/
def selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [Fintype V]
    {c s m E P : Nat}
    (hdim : c + s <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V)
    (C : ActualSourceStarLaw.CenterTable (V := V) c)
    (T : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable (V := V) (c + s))
    (f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) V) : Prop :=
  let betaRat := matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim C f
  let X := matchingStarMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim C T f
  let M : Rat := 2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - (c + m * s))
  let B : Rat := 2 ^ (Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V - c)
  let F : Rat := 2 ^ Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V
  let beta : Real := (betaRat : Real)
  let signal : Real := (((successMargin E / 4) * (M / F) : Rat) : Real)
  (successMargin E / 4 * (M / B) * betaRat +
      successMargin E / 4 * (M / F) <= X) /\
    X <= betaRat /\
    successMargin E / 4 * (M / F) <= betaRat /\
    beta ^ (-(m : Real) / (P : Real)) <=
      signal ^ (-(m : Real) / (P : Real))

def selected_actual_coordinate_score_bundle
    {V : Type*} [AddCommGroup V] [Module (ZMod 2) V] [Fintype V]
    {c s m E : Nat}
    (hdim : c + s <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V)
    (C : ActualSourceStarLaw.CenterTable (V := V) c)
    (T : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable (V := V) (c + s)) : Prop :=
  successMargin E <= StarDensity (V := V) (k := m)
    (grass_nonempty_of_le (by
      calc
        c <= c + s := Nat.le_add_right c s
        _ <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) V := hdim))
    (fun K => extension_nonempty K (Nat.le_add_right c s) hdim) C T

set_option maxHeartbeats 800000 in
/-- The actual low-HC and high-spectral terms for one fixed coordinate
functional are strictly smaller than the two signals returned for that same
functional by the weighted-star selector.  The low theorem pays for the
outer factor two; the high tail and threshold together consume at most half
of the additive signal.  No final RHS comparison is an input. -/
theorem selected_actual_fixed_functional_rhs_lt_matching_mass
    {n c s h m J E : Nat} {rho : Real} {e : Rat}
    (C : ActualSourceStarLaw.CenterTable (V := Fin n -> ZMod 2) c)
    (T : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable (V := Fin n -> ZMod 2) (c + s))
    (f : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) (Fin n -> ZMod 2))
    (hm : 256 <= m) (hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h)
    (hcNat : c = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m h)
    (hsNat : s = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h)
    (hEven : c + s = 2 * h)
    (hdiv : ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m ∣ h)
    (hE : E = ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
    (hrd : sourceRankExponent m < c + s)
    (he0 : 0 <= e) (he : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m h)
    (hfail : forall (q : Nat) (Q : Grass (Fin n -> ZMod 2) q)
      (P : ActualMaximalPairLadder.DecodedPair Q (c + s)),
      q + ActualMaximalPairLadder.codim P.W = sourceRankExponent m ->
        Ne (Fintype.card (ActualMaximalPairLadder.Zoom Q P)) 0 ->
          ActualMaximalPairLadder.agreement (fun L => T L) Q P <= e)
    (hc : (c : Real) = 2 * (1 - rho) * (h : Real))
    (hs : (s : Real) = 2 * rho * (h : Real))
    (hrho : (fixedRho m : Real) = rho)
    (hdim : c + m * s <= n)
    (hmassdim : c + s <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n -> ZMod 2))
    (hselector :
      successMargin E / 4 *
          ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ (n - c)) *
          matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim C f +
        successMargin E / 4 *
          ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n) <=
        matchingStarMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim C T f)
    (haddBeta : successMargin E / 4 *
        ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n) <=
      matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim C f)
    (hHC : HC46ExactContract)
    (hJ : h ^ 2 < J) (hN : n = 2 * J) :
    2 * selected_actual_analytic_rhs (n := n) (c := c) (s := s) (m := m)
        C T f (sourceRankExponent m) (sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real))
        (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) <
      (matchingStarMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim C T f : Real) := by
  let betaRat := matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim C f
  let beta : Real := (betaRat : Real)
  let X : Rat := matchingStarMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim C T f
  let firstRat := successMargin E / 4 *
    ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ (n - c)) * betaRat
  let additiveRat := successMargin E / 4 *
    ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n)
  have hbetaEq := matching_center_mass_eq_grassmann_beta
    (V := Fin n -> ZMod 2) (c := c) (s := s) (m := m) hmassdim C f
  have hcenterNonempty : Nonempty (Grass (Fin n -> ZMod 2) c) :=
    grass_nonempty_of_le (by
      calc
        c <= c + s := Nat.le_add_right c s
        _ <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n -> ZMod 2) := hmassdim)
  have hcenterCardPos :
      (0 : Real) < (Fintype.card (Grass (Fin n -> ZMod 2) c) : Real) := by
    exact_mod_cast (Fintype.card_pos_iff.mpr hcenterNonempty)
  have hbeta0 : 0 <= beta := by
    dsimp [beta, betaRat]
    rw [hbetaEq]
    apply div_nonneg
    · exact Finset.sum_nonneg fun R _ => by split <;> positivity
    · exact hcenterCardPos.le
  have hbeta1 : beta <= 1 := by
    dsimp [beta, betaRat]
    exact_mod_cast (selected_matching_center_mass_le_one
      (V := Fin n -> ZMod 2) (c := c) (s := s) (m := m)
      hmassdim C f)
  have hbetaFloor : (2 : Real) ^ sourceLowBetaFloorExponent m h <= beta := by
    have hsignal := selected_beta_signal_eq_source_floor
      (m := m) (h := h) (n := n) (c := c) (s := s) (E := E)
      (by omega) hdiv hE hdim hrho hc hs
    have haddReal : (additiveRat : Real) <= beta := by
      dsimp [additiveRat, beta, betaRat]
      exact_mod_cast haddBeta
    rw [← hsignal]
    exact haddReal
  have hlow := selected_fixed_functional_low_HC_signal_bound
    (T := T) (f := f) hm hfloor hEven hrd he0 he hfail hbetaFloor hHC
  have hLowTerm :
      2 * ((2 : Real) ^ (m : Real) *
        beta ^ (1 - (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
        (selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s)
          (r := sourceRankExponent m) (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real))) ^ m) <=
        (firstRat : Real) := by
    have hfirst := selected_actual_first_signal_eq_dyadic
      (m := m) (h := h) (n := n) (c := c) (s := s) (E := E)
      (by omega) hdiv hE hdim hrho hs
    have hfirstRat : (firstRat : Real) =
        (2 : Real) ^ (sourceLowTargetSignalExponent m h) * beta := by
      dsimp [firstRat, beta, betaRat]
      rw [Rat.cast_mul, hfirst]
    have hlowSignal := hlow.2
    rw [← hfirstRat] at hlowSignal
    simpa [pow_succ, mul_assoc, mul_left_comm, mul_comm] using hlowSignal
  have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
  have hlarge := (low_HC_height_floor_dominates_m_squared hmpos).trans hfloor
  have hm1 : 1 <= m := by omega
  have hmSq : m ^ 2 <= m ^ 4 := by
    have hsq : 1 <= m ^ 2 := Nat.one_le_pow 2 m hm1
    calc
      m ^ 2 = m ^ 2 * 1 := by simp
      _ <= m ^ 2 * m ^ 2 := Nat.mul_le_mul_left _ hsq
      _ = m ^ 4 := by ring
  have hmLarge : 20001 * m ^ 2 <= (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 := by
    nlinarith [hmSq]
  have h20001 : 20001 * m ^ 2 <= h := hmLarge.trans hlarge
  have h4000 : 4000 * m ^ 2 <= h := by
    have hmSqOne : 1 <= m ^ 2 := Nat.one_le_pow 2 m hm1
    have hp : 1 <= 4000 * m ^ 2 := by
      calc
        1 <= m ^ 2 := hmSqOne
        _ <= 4000 * m ^ 2 := by
          simpa only [one_mul] using
            Nat.mul_le_mul_right (m ^ 2) (by omega : 1 <= 4000)
    have hle : 4000 * m ^ 2 <= (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 := by nlinarith
    exact hle.trans hlarge
  have h12Large : 12 * m <= (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 := by
    nlinarith [hmSq]
  have h12 : 12 * m <= h := h12Large.trans hlarge
  have hsFixed : (s : Real) = 2 * (fixedRho m : Real) * (h : Real) := by
    rw [hrho]
    exact hs
  have htailGuards := source_tail_guards_of_growth hm h4000 h12 hJ hsFixed
  have hTailB : 1 <= (s : Real) - 1 := htailGuards.1
  have hTailN : ((c + s : Nat) : Real) - (n : Real) <=
      -(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1) - 3 := by
    simpa [hEven, hN, Nat.cast_mul] using htailGuards.2
  have hTail := selected_actual_high_spectral_sum_le_tail
    (n := n) (d := c + s) (r := sourceRankExponent m) (s := s)
    (selectedF T f) hTailB hTailN
  have hhigh := selected_actual_high_error_budget_le_actual_additive_half
    (m := m) (h := h) (n := n)
    (c := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m h) (E := E) hm
    h20001
    hdiv hE (by simpa [hcNat, hsNat] using hdim) hrho
    (by simpa [hcNat] using hc) (by simpa [hsNat] using hs) hbeta0 hbeta1
  have hadditiveSame :
      ((((successMargin E / 4) *
        ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n) : Rat) : Rat) : Real) =
        (additiveRat : Real) := by
    rfl
  have hXcast : (X : Real) =
      (matchingStarMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim C T f : Real) := rfl
  have hselectorReal : (firstRat : Real) + (additiveRat : Real) <= (X : Real) := by
    dsimp [firstRat, additiveRat, betaRat, X]
    exact_mod_cast hselector
  have haddPos : 0 < (additiveRat : Real) := by
    have hsignal := selected_beta_signal_eq_source_floor
      (m := m) (h := h) (n := n) (c := c) (s := s) (E := E)
      (by omega) hdiv hE hdim hrho hc hs
    change 0 < ((((successMargin E / 4) *
      ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n) : Rat) : Real))
    rw [hsignal]
    exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _
  let lowTerm := (2 : Real) ^ (m : Real) * beta ^
    (1 - (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real)) *
    (selectedLowHC46NormBound (d := c + s) (r := sourceRankExponent m)
      (p := sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real))) ^ m
  let thresholdTerm := (2 : Real) ^ (m : Real) *
    (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ m * beta
  let highTerm := (1 / (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2) *
    Finset.sum (selectedHighFinIndexSet (c + s) (sourceRankExponent m))
      (fun i => ((2 : Real) ^ (-(i.val : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) +
        3 * (2 : Real) ^ ((i.val : Real) - (n : Real))) *
        uniformMean (fun W : BinaryMatrix n (c + s) =>
          (rankProjection i.val (indicator (selectedF T f)) W) ^ 2))
  have hRhsEq : selected_actual_analytic_rhs (n := n) (c := c) (s := s) (m := m)
      C T f (sourceRankExponent m) (sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real))
      (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) = lowTerm + thresholdTerm + highTerm := by
    unfold selected_actual_analytic_rhs
    rw [← hbetaEq]
    have hPposNat : 0 < sourceMomentP m := by
      unfold sourceMomentP
      exact Nat.pow_pos (by omega)
    have hmRpos : (0 : Real) < (m : Real) := by
      exact_mod_cast (show 0 < m by omega)
    have hPpos : (0 : Real) < (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
      exact_mod_cast hPposNat
    have hInv : ((sourceMomentP m : Real) / (m : Real))⁻¹ =
        (m : Real) / (sourceMomentP m : Real) := by
      field_simp [ne_of_gt hmRpos, ne_of_gt hPpos]
    rw [hInv]
    dsimp [lowTerm, thresholdTerm, highTerm, beta, betaRat]
    rw [← Real.rpow_natCast (2 : Real) m]
    ring
  have htailTerm : highTerm <=
      (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
        (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 := by
    dsimp [highTerm]
    have hcoef : 0 <= (1 / (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 : Real) := by
      positivity
    calc
      (1 / (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2) *
          Finset.sum (selectedHighFinIndexSet (c + s) (sourceRankExponent m))
            (fun i => ((2 : Real) ^ (-(i.val : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) +
              3 * (2 : Real) ^ ((i.val : Real) - (n : Real))) *
              uniformMean (fun W : BinaryMatrix n (c + s) =>
                (rankProjection i.val (indicator (selectedF T f)) W) ^ 2)) <=
        (1 / (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2) *
          (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) :=
        mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hTail hcoef
      _ = (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
          (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 := by ring
  have hhighActual : 2 * (thresholdTerm + highTerm) <= (additiveRat : Real) / 2 := by
    have haPos : 0 < manuscriptMomentThreshold m h := by
      unfold manuscriptMomentThreshold
      exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _
    have htail := htailTerm
    have htailNonneg : 0 <=
        (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) := by
      positivity
    have htailOver0 : 0 <=
        (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
          (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 :=
      div_nonneg htailNonneg (sq_nonneg (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h))
    have hlargeTail :
        (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
          (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 <=
        2 * (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
          (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 := by
      calc
        _ <= _ + _ := le_add_of_nonneg_right htailOver0
        _ = _ := by ring
    calc
      2 * (thresholdTerm + highTerm) <=
          2 * (thresholdTerm +
            (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
              (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2) := by
        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left
          (add_le_add le_rfl htail) (by norm_num)
      _ <= 2 * (2 *
          (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
            (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 + thresholdTerm) := by
        have hsum : thresholdTerm +
            (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
              (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 <=
            2 * (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
              (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 + thresholdTerm := by
          calc
            _ <= thresholdTerm + 2 *
                (2 : Real) ^ (-(sourceRankExponent m : Real) * ((s : Real) - 1)) /
                  (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) ^ 2 := add_le_add le_rfl hlargeTail
            _ = _ := by ring
        exact mul_le_mul_of_nonneg_left hsum (by norm_num)
      _ <= (additiveRat : Real) / 2 := by
        have hconvert :
            ((((successMargin E / 4) *
              ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h)) /
                (2 : Rat) ^ n) : Rat) : Rat) : Real) =
              (additiveRat : Real) := by
          simpa [hcNat, hsNat] using hadditiveSame
        rw [hconvert.symm]
        simpa [thresholdTerm, hcNat, hsNat, add_comm] using hhigh
  have hRhsComponents :
      2 * selected_actual_analytic_rhs (n := n) (c := c) (s := s) (m := m)
        C T f (sourceRankExponent m) (sourceMomentP m) (2 * (e : Real))
        (manuscriptMomentThreshold m h) <=
        (firstRat : Real) + (additiveRat : Real) / 2 := by
    rw [hRhsEq]
    calc
      2 * (lowTerm + thresholdTerm + highTerm) =
          2 * lowTerm + 2 * (thresholdTerm + highTerm) := by ring
      _ <= (firstRat : Real) + (additiveRat : Real) / 2 :=
        add_le_add hLowTerm hhighActual
  have hstrict : (firstRat : Real) + (additiveRat : Real) / 2 < (X : Real) := by
    linarith [hselectorReal, haddPos]
  exact lt_of_le_of_lt hRhsComponents hstrict


theorem selected_margin_source_dimension
    {N rows L samplerA m : Nat} (I : ActualOccurrenceAllocation.Instance N rows)
    (copies : Nat)
    (U : TaggedGoodU I copies (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
      (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
    (A : SideComplement I copies U) (cutoff : Real -> Nat)
    (hsel : ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector
      (fun j => max
        (ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
          (fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m) cutoff j) (j + 2)) L =
        (m : WithBot Nat))
    (hA : 1 <= samplerA) :
    ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) +
      ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) <=
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 := by
  have hs := ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector_spec hsel
  rcases hs.1 with ⟨hm, _, _, _, _, _, _, hcut, _⟩
  let h := ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m
  let J := SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA h
  have hh : m + 2 <= h := (Nat.le_max_right _ _).trans hcut
  have hp : h ^ 2 < J := by
    dsimp [J, h]
    simpa only [one_mul] using
      ((Nat.mul_le_mul_right ((ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) ^ 2) hA).trans_lt
        (SamplerParameters.numerator_lt_blocks samplerA
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
  have hpos : 0 < h := by omega
  have hle : h <= h ^ 2 := by simpa [pow_two] using Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left h hpos
  have hsplit := ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.leaf_split_total (m := m) (h := h)
  have hsmall : h <= J := by omega
  rw [ActualTaggedComplementIncidence.sideComplement_finrank I copies U A]
  change ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m h +
      ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h <= 2 * J
  omega

theorem selected_margin_coordinate_dimension
    {N rows L samplerA m : Nat} (I : ActualOccurrenceAllocation.Instance N rows)
    (copies : Nat)
    (U : TaggedGoodU I copies (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
      (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
    (A : SideComplement I copies U) (cutoff : Real -> Nat)
    (hsel : ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector
      (fun j => max
        (ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
          (fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m) cutoff j) (j + 2)) L =
        (m : WithBot Nat))
    (hA : 1 <= samplerA)
    (hsource :
      ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) +
        ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) <=
        Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1) :
    ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) +
      ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) <=
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2)
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient
          (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))) :=
  ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.actualCoordinateDimensionBound
    I copies U A hsource

set_option maxHeartbeats 800000 in
set_option diagnostics true in
/-- The original side score and the original full failed-zoom family feed one
and the same selected complement and functional.  The failure family is
transported to that complement's coordinate leaf, and the fixed large dyadic
P9C caller then supplies the actual star-mass-to-moment bound at the same
source functional.  The selector base explicitly includes the analytic
margin floor; no final margin, beta, PR, or moment estimate is an input. -/
theorem original_score_and_failure_select_same_actual_moment_witness
    {N rows : Nat} (I : ActualOccurrenceAllocation.Instance N rows)
    (copies : Nat) {L samplerA : Nat} {m : Nat}
    (U : TaggedGoodU I copies (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
      (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
    (C : ActualTaggedFixedTableAcceptance.TaggedCenterTable I copies)
    (T : ActualTaggedFixedTableAcceptance.TaggedLeafTable I copies)
    (cutoff : Real -> Nat)
    (hsel : ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector
      (fun j => max
        (ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
          (fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m) cutoff j) (j + 2)) L =
        (m : WithBot Nat))
    (hA : 1 <= samplerA)
    (hscore : ActualTaggedMZSideDraw.sideConditionalDensity I copies U
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) m C T >=
      ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.successMargin
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
    (e : Rat)
    (hfailSource : forall (A : SideComplement I copies U)
      (q : Nat) (Q : Grass A.1 q)
      (P : ActualMaximalPairLadder.DecodedPair Q
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) +
          ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))),
      q + ActualMaximalPairLadder.codim P.W = sourceRankExponent m ->
        Ne (Fintype.card (ActualMaximalPairLadder.Zoom Q P)) 0 ->
          ActualMaximalPairLadder.agreement
            (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedLeafTable
              I copies U A T) Q P <= e)
    (he : manuscriptAgreementFloor m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) < (e : Real))
    (he' : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
    (hHC : ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticMoment.HC46ExactContract)
    (hSpectral : ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticMoment.Spectral47ExactContract cutoff) :
    exists A : SideComplement I copies U,
    let sourceDim :
        ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) +
          ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) <=
        Module.finrank (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
      selected_margin_source_dimension (N := N) (rows := rows)
        (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA) (m := m) (I := I)
        (copies := copies) (U := U) (A := A) (cutoff := cutoff)
        (hsel := hsel) (hA := hA)
    let coordDim :
        ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) +
          ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) <=
        Module.finrank (ZMod 2)
          (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient
            (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
              (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))) :=
      selected_margin_coordinate_dimension (N := N) (rows := rows)
        (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA) (m := m) (I := I)
        (copies := copies) (U := U) (A := A) (cutoff := cutoff)
        (hsel := hsel) (hA := hA) sourceDim
    exists fCoord : Module.Dual (ZMod 2)
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient
        (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))),
    selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle
      (m := m)
      (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
      (P := ActualOriginalFailureFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP m)
      coordDim
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable I copies U A C)
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable I copies U A T)
      fCoord /\
    selected_actual_coordinate_score_bundle
      (m := m)
      (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
      coordDim
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable I copies U A C)
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable I copies U A T) /\
    exists fSource : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) A.1,
      ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.coordinateFunctional I copies U A fSource =
        fCoord /\
      (ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.matchingStarMass
        (V := A.1) (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right
          (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
          (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
        sourceDim
        (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedCenterTable I copies U A C)
        (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedLeafTable I copies U A T)
        fSource) =
      (ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.matchingStarMass
        (V := ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient
          (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))) (m := m)
        (Nat.le_add_right
          (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
          (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
        coordDim
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable I copies U A C)
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable I copies U A T)
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.coordinateFunctional I copies U A fSource)) /\
      (ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.matchingStarMass
        (V := A.1) (m := m)
        (Nat.le_add_right
          (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
          (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
        sourceDim
        (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedCenterTable I copies U A C)
        (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedLeafTable I copies U A T)
        fSource : Real) <=
        2 * ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticMoment.selected_actual_analytic_rhs
          (n := 2 * SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
          (c := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
          (s := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
          (m := m)
          (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable
            I copies U A C)
          (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable
            I copies U A T)
          (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.coordinateFunctional I copies U A fSource)
          (sourceRankExponent m) (ActualOriginalFailureRowGenericFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP m)
          (2 * (e : Real))
          (ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticNumerics.manuscriptMomentThreshold m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)) := by
  let base : Nat -> Nat := fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m
  let h := ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m
  let J := SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA h
  let c := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m h
  let s := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h
  have hparams := ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.selected_spectral_parameters
    base cutoff hsel
  rcases hparams with ⟨hm256, hh, hdiv, hhpos, hspos, hsplit, hrhoPos,
    hrho, hcReal, hsReal, hbaseFloor, hcutFloor⟩
  have hJ : h ^ 2 < J := selected_actual_height_square_lt_blocks base cutoff hsel hA
  have hguardBase := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.selected_fixedRho_quotient_dimension_guard
    (sourceHMin := fun j => ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
      (fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m) cutoff j)
    (L := L) (m := m) (A := samplerA) hA hsel
  have hsplit' : c + s = 2 * h := by simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit
  have hspos' : 0 < s := by simpa [s, h] using hspos
  have hguard : m * (2 * h - c) +
      ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h + 2 <= 2 * J - c := by
    have hsub : 2 * h - c = s := by omega
    simpa [J, h, c, s, hsub] using hguardBase
  have hP : 0 < ActualOriginalFailureFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP m := by
    unfold ActualOriginalFailureFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP
    positivity
  have htle : c <= 2 * h := by omega
  have hle : h <= h ^ 2 := by
    simpa [pow_two] using Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left h hhpos
  have hhJ : h <= J := hle.trans_lt hJ |>.le
  obtain ⟨A, fCoord, hweighted, hdom, hbeta, hholder, hscoreCoord⟩ :=
    actual_side_score_selects_same_coordinate_functional
      (I := I) (copies := copies) (U := U)
      (t := c) (h := h) (k := m)
      (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
      (P := ActualOriginalFailureFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP m)
      htle hhJ C T (by omega) hguard hscore hP
  let fSource : Module.Dual (ZMod 2) A.1 :=
    fCoord.comp
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.actualCoordinateEquiv I copies U A).toLinearMap
  have hcoord : ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.coordinateFunctional
      I copies U A fSource = fCoord := by
    ext x
    simp [fSource, ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.coordinateFunctional]
  have hsourceDim := selected_margin_source_dimension
    (N := N) (rows := rows) (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA) (m := m)
    (I := I) (copies := copies) (U := U) (A := A)
    (cutoff := cutoff) (hsel := hsel) (hA := hA)
  have hcoordDim := selected_margin_coordinate_dimension
    (N := N) (rows := rows) (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA) (m := m)
    (I := I) (copies := copies) (U := U) (A := A)
    (cutoff := cutoff) (hsel := hsel) (hA := hA) hsourceDim
  have hmassCoord := ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.matchingStarMass_actual_coordinate
    (m := m) I copies U A (Nat.le_add_right c s) hsourceDim C T fSource
  have hsplit' : c + s = 2 * h := by simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit
  have hsub : 2 * h - c = s := by omega
  have hsplitCoord : c + (2 * h - c) = 2 * h :=
    Nat.add_sub_of_le (by omega)
  let BundleIndex := {u : Nat // c + u <=
    Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J)}
  let bundleAt : BundleIndex -> Prop := fun i =>
    selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle
      (m := m) (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
      (P := ActualOriginalFailureFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP m) i.property
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable
        (t := c) I copies U A C)
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable
        (d := c + i.val) I copies U A T) fCoord
  have hdimWide : c + (2 * h - c) <=
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) := by
    simpa [hsub] using hcoordDim
  let bundleTarget : BundleIndex := ⟨s, hcoordDim⟩
  let bundleWide : BundleIndex := ⟨2 * h - c, hdimWide⟩
  have hbundleIndex : bundleTarget = bundleWide := by
    apply Subtype.ext
    dsimp [bundleTarget, bundleWide]
    omega
  have hsignalBundle : selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle
      (m := m) (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
      (P := ActualOriginalFailureFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP m) hcoordDim
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable I copies U A C)
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable I copies U A T)
      fCoord := by
    have htuple := And.intro hweighted (And.intro hdom (And.intro hbeta hholder))
    have hcoordFinrankWide :
        Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) =
          2 * J := by
      simpa [ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient] using
        (Module.finrank_fin_fun (R := ZMod 2) (n := 2 * J))
    have htupleWide : bundleAt bundleWide := by
      change selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle
        (m := m) (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
        (P := ActualOriginalFailureFixedMomentCaller.fixedMomentP m) hdimWide
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable
          (t := c) I copies U A C)
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable
          (d := c + (2 * h - c)) I copies U A T) fCoord
      simpa only [selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle, hcoordFinrankWide] using htuple
    have htupleTarget : bundleAt bundleTarget :=
      (congrArg bundleAt hbundleIndex).symm ▸ htupleWide
    simpa [bundleAt, bundleTarget, selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle] using
      htupleTarget
  let ScoreIndex := {d : Nat // c <= d /\ d <=
    Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J)}
  let scoreAt : ScoreIndex -> Prop := fun i =>
    successMargin (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) <=
      ActualChangedAmbient8SBoundary.StarDensity (V :=
        ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) (k := m)
        (grass_nonempty_of_le (i.property.1.trans i.property.2))
        (fun K => extension_nonempty K i.property.1 i.property.2)
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable
          (t := c) I copies U A C)
        (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable
          (d := i.val) I copies U A T)
  let scoreTarget : ScoreIndex := ⟨c + s, Nat.le_add_right c s, hcoordDim⟩
  have hdScoreWide : 2 * h <=
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) := by
    have hleSq : h <= h ^ 2 := by
      simpa [pow_two] using Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left h hhpos
    have hleJ : h <= J := hleSq.trans_lt hJ |>.le
    calc
      2 * h <= 2 * J := Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 hleJ
      _ = Module.finrank (ZMod 2)
          (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) := by
        symm
        simpa [ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient] using
          (Module.finrank_fin_fun (R := ZMod 2) (n := 2 * J))
  have hdScoreSource : c + (2 * h - c) <=
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) := by
    rw [hsplitCoord]
    exact hdScoreWide
  let scoreSource : ScoreIndex :=
    ⟨c + (2 * h - c), Nat.le_add_right c (2 * h - c), hdScoreSource⟩
  have hscoreBundle : selected_actual_coordinate_score_bundle
      (m := m) (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) hcoordDim
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable I copies U A C)
      (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable I copies U A T) := by
    have hscoreAtSource : scoreAt scoreSource := by
      change successMargin (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) <=
        ActualChangedAmbient8SBoundary.StarDensity
          (V := ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) (k := m)
          (grass_nonempty_of_le (Nat.le_add_right c (2 * h - c) |>.trans hdScoreSource))
          (fun K => extension_nonempty K (Nat.le_add_right c (2 * h - c)) hdScoreSource)
          (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable
            (t := c) I copies U A C)
          (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable
            (d := c + (2 * h - c)) I copies U A T)
      simpa [c, h, J, ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient] using hscoreCoord
    have hscoreSourceTarget : scoreSource = scoreTarget := by
      apply Subtype.ext
      dsimp [scoreSource, scoreTarget]
      omega
    have hscoreAtTarget : scoreAt scoreTarget := hscoreSourceTarget ▸ hscoreAtSource
    simpa [scoreAt, scoreTarget, selected_actual_coordinate_score_bundle,
      c, s, h, J, ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient] using
      hscoreAtTarget
  have hfloorPos : 0 < manuscriptAgreementFloor m h := by
    unfold manuscriptAgreementFloor
    exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _
  have hePos : 0 < (e : Real) := hfloorPos.trans he
  have he0 : (0 : Rat) <= e := by exact_mod_cast hePos.le
  refine ⟨A, fCoord, hsignalBundle, hscoreBundle, fSource, hcoord, ?_, ?_⟩
  · exact hmassCoord
  have hfixed := ActualOriginalFailureRowGenericFixedMomentCaller.selected_actual_fixed_moment_bound_from_source_failure
    (N := N) (rows := rows) (m := m) (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA)
    I copies U A C T fSource base cutoff hsel hA (sourceRankExponent m)
    (by
      have hm : 0 < m := by omega
      have hdom := low_HC_height_floor_dominates_m_squared hm
      have hm1 : 1 <= m := by omega
      have hm3le4 : m ^ 3 <= m ^ 4 := by
        calc
          m ^ 3 = m ^ 3 * 1 := by simp
          _ <= m ^ 3 * m := Nat.mul_le_mul_left (m ^ 3) hm1
          _ = m ^ 4 := by ring
      have hbig : sourceRankExponent m < 2 * (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 := by
        dsimp [sourceRankExponent]
        nlinarith [hm3le4]
      have hbase : sourceMarginHeightFloor m <= h := by
        simpa [base, h] using hbaseFloor
      have hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h :=
        (Nat.le_max_right _ _).trans hbase
      have hscaled := Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 hdom
      have hheight := Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 hfloor
      have hsplit' : c + s = 2 * h := by
        simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit
      dsimp [c, s, ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT,
        ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK] at hsplit' ⊢
      omega)
    e he0 (hfailSource A) hHC hSpectral
    (ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticNumerics.manuscriptMomentThreshold m h)
    (Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _)
  rcases hfixed with ⟨_, _, _, _, _, _, hmass⟩
  simpa [hcoord, h, ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock,
    SamplerParameters.blocks] using hmass

set_option diagnostics true in
theorem original_score_and_failure_same_witness_contradiction
    {N rows : Nat} (I : ActualOccurrenceAllocation.Instance N rows)
    (copies : Nat) {L samplerA : Nat} {m : Nat}
    (U : TaggedGoodU I copies (SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA
      (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
    (C : ActualTaggedFixedTableAcceptance.TaggedCenterTable I copies)
    (T : ActualTaggedFixedTableAcceptance.TaggedLeafTable I copies)
    (cutoff : Real -> Nat)
    (hsel : ActualCmmsaAdmissibilitySelector.selector
      (fun j => max
        (ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
          (fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m) cutoff j) (j + 2)) L =
        (m : WithBot Nat))
    (hA : 1 <= samplerA)
    (hscore : ActualTaggedMZSideDraw.sideConditionalDensity I copies U
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) m C T >=
      ActualStarAcceptedGoodMass.successMargin
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m
          (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m)))
    (e : Rat)
    (hfailSource : forall (A : SideComplement I copies U)
      (q : Nat) (Q : Grass A.1 q)
      (P : ActualMaximalPairLadder.DecodedPair Q
        (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) +
          ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m
            (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))),
      q + ActualMaximalPairLadder.codim P.W = sourceRankExponent m ->
        Ne (Fintype.card (ActualMaximalPairLadder.Zoom Q P)) 0 ->
          ActualMaximalPairLadder.agreement
            (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedLeafTable
              I copies U A T) Q P <= e)
    (he : manuscriptAgreementFloor m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m) < (e : Real))
    (he' : (e : Real) < 2 * manuscriptAgreementFloor m
        (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m))
    (hHC : ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticMoment.HC46ExactContract)
    (hSpectral : ActualSelectedComplementAnalyticMoment.Spectral47ExactContract cutoff) :
    False := by
  have hresult := original_score_and_failure_select_same_actual_moment_witness
    (I := I) (copies := copies) (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA) (m := m)
    U C T cutoff hsel hA hscore e hfailSource he he' hHC hSpectral
  rcases hresult with ⟨A, fCoord, hsignalBundle, _, fSource, hcoord,
    hmassEq, hmassBound⟩
  let base : Nat → Nat := fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m
  let h := ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock L m
  let J := SamplerParameters.blocks samplerA h
  let c := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT m h
  let s := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK m h
  let n := 2 * J
  have hparams := ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.selected_spectral_parameters
    base cutoff hsel
  rcases hparams with ⟨hm256, hh, hdiv, hhpos, hspos, hsplit, hrhoPos,
    hrho, hcReal, hsReal, hbaseFloor, hcutFloor⟩
  have hsplit' : c + s = 2 * h := by simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit
  have hsub : 2 * h - c = s := by omega
  have hJ : h ^ 2 < J := selected_actual_height_square_lt_blocks base cutoff hsel hA
  have hcLe : c ≤ 2 * J := by
    have hleSq : h ≤ h ^ 2 := by
      simpa [pow_two] using Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left h hhpos
    have hleJ : h ≤ J := hleSq.trans_lt hJ |>.le
    have hsplit' : c + s = 2 * h := by simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit
    calc
      c ≤ c + s := Nat.le_add_right c s
      _ = 2 * h := hsplit'
      _ ≤ 2 * J := Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 hleJ
  have hbase : sourceMarginHeightFloor m ≤ h := by
    simpa [base, h] using hbaseFloor
  have hLowFloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m ≤ h := by
    calc
      sourceLowHCHeightFloor m ≤ sourceMarginHeightFloor m := Nat.le_max_right _ _
      _ ≤ h := hbase
  have hdim : c + m * s ≤ n := by
    have hguardBase := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.selected_fixedRho_quotient_dimension_guard
      (sourceHMin := fun j => ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.analyticSourceHeightFloor
        (fun _ => sourceMarginHeightFloor m) cutoff j)
      (L := L) (m := m) (A := samplerA) hA hsel
    have hsplit' : c + s = 2 * h := by simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit
    have hspos' : 0 < s := by simpa [s, h] using hspos
    have hsub : 2 * h - c = s := by omega
    have hguard : m * s +
        ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h + 2 ≤ 2 * J - c := by
      simpa [J, h, c, s, hsub] using hguardBase
    have hguardFull : c + m * s +
        ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h + 2 ≤ 2 * J := by
      simpa only [Nat.add_assoc, Nat.add_comm, Nat.add_left_comm] using
        (Nat.le_sub_iff_add_le hcLe).mp hguard
    dsimp [n, J]
    omega
  have hmassdim : c + s <= Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n -> ZMod 2) := by
    have hsMul : s <= m * s := Nat.le_mul_of_pos_left s (by omega)
    have hnatDim : c + s <= n := by omega
    have hn : Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (Fin n -> ZMod 2) = n :=
      Module.finrank_fin_fun (R := ZMod 2) (n := n)
    simpa only [hn] using hnatDim
  have hsourceDim := selected_margin_source_dimension
    (N := N) (rows := rows) (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA) (m := m)
    (I := I) (copies := copies) (U := U) (A := A)
    (cutoff := cutoff) (hsel := hsel) (hA := hA)
  have hcoordDim := selected_margin_coordinate_dimension
    (N := N) (rows := rows) (L := L) (samplerA := samplerA) (m := m)
    (I := I) (copies := copies) (U := U) (A := A)
    (cutoff := cutoff) (hsel := hsel) (hA := hA) hsourceDim
  have hrd : sourceRankExponent m < c + s := by
    have hmpos : 0 < m := by omega
    have hm1 : 1 <= m := by omega
    have hm3le4 : m ^ 3 <= m ^ 4 := by
      calc
        m ^ 3 = m ^ 3 * 1 := by simp
        _ <= m ^ 3 * m := Nat.mul_le_mul_left (m ^ 3) hm1
        _ = m ^ 4 := by ring
    have hbig : sourceRankExponent m < 2 * (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 := by
      dsimp [sourceRankExponent]
      nlinarith [hm3le4]
    have hbase : sourceMarginHeightFloor m <= h := by
      simpa [base, h] using hbaseFloor
    have hfloor : sourceLowHCHeightFloor m <= h :=
      (Nat.le_max_right _ _).trans hbase
    have hlowLarge := low_HC_height_floor_dominates_m_squared hmpos
    have hheight : 2 * (4000 * m ^ 2) ^ 2 <= 2 * h :=
      Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 (hlowLarge.trans hfloor)
    have hsplit' : c + s = 2 * h := by simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit
    omega
  have he0 : (0 : Rat) <= e := by
    have hfpos : 0 < manuscriptAgreementFloor m h := by
      unfold manuscriptAgreementFloor
      exact Real.rpow_pos_of_pos (by norm_num) _
    have hepos : 0 < (e : Real) := hfpos.trans he
    exact_mod_cast hepos.le
  have hfailCoord :=
    ActualOriginalFailureAnalyticCaller.source_failed_zoom_to_selected_coordinate
      I U A T e he0 (hfailSource A)
  let Cc : ActualSourceStarLaw.CenterTable
      (V := Fin n -> ZMod 2) c :=
    ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateCenterTable
      (t := c) I copies U A C
  let Tc : ActualSourceStarLaw.LeafTable
      (V := Fin n -> ZMod 2) (c + s) :=
    ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.selectedCoordinateLeafTable
      (d := c + s) I copies U A T
  let fc := ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.coordinateFunctional I copies U A fSource
  have hfail : forall (q : Nat) (Q : Grass (Fin n → ZMod 2) q)
      (P : ActualMaximalPairLadder.DecodedPair Q (c + s)),
      q + ActualMaximalPairLadder.codim P.W = sourceRankExponent m →
        Ne (Fintype.card (ActualMaximalPairLadder.Zoom Q P)) 0 →
          ActualMaximalPairLadder.agreement (fun L => Tc L) Q P ≤ e := by
    simpa [ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient,
      ActualLeafLabelRankImageAlignment.Ambient, n, J, c, s, Tc] using hfailCoord
  have hscoreEq : fc = fCoord := by
    simpa [fc] using hcoord
  have hbundle := hsignalBundle
  dsimp [selected_actual_weighted_signal_bundle] at hbundle
  rcases hbundle with ⟨hselector, _, haddBeta, _⟩
  have hcoordFinrank :
      Module.finrank (ZMod 2) (ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) = n := by
    simpa [ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient, n, J] using
      (Module.finrank_fin_fun (R := ZMod 2) (n := 2 * J))
  have hselectorNorm :
      (successMargin (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) / 4 *
          ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ (n - c))) *
          matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim Cc fCoord +
        successMargin (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) / 4 *
           ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n) <=
        matchingStarMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim Cc Tc fCoord := by
    have hselector' := hselector
    rw [hcoordFinrank] at hselector'
    simpa [n, c, s, h, J, Cc, Tc, ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock,
      SamplerParameters.blocks, ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT,
      ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK, hsplit', hsub] using hselector'
  have haddBetaNorm :
      successMargin (ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h) / 4 *
           ((2 : Rat) ^ (n - (c + m * s)) / (2 : Rat) ^ n) <=
        matchingCenterMass (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s) hmassdim Cc fCoord := by
    have haddBeta' := haddBeta
    rw [hcoordFinrank] at haddBeta'
    simpa [n, c, s, h, J, Cc, Tc, ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.hBlock,
      SamplerParameters.blocks, ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafT,
      ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.leafK, hsplit', hsub] using haddBeta'
  have hrhoFixed : (fixedRho m : Real) =
      ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.actualSelectedRho m h := by
    have hfixedRho := ActualMZ24FixedRhoPointwiseSelector.fixedRho_eq_inverse_bOf
      (m := m) (by omega)
    have hfixedReal : (fixedRho m : Real) = 1 / (ActualCmmsaParameterReconciliation.bOf m : Real) := by
      have hcast := congrArg (fun q : Rat => (q : Real)) hfixedRho
      simpa [Rat.cast_div, Rat.cast_natCast] using hcast
    rw [hfixedReal, hrho]
  have hstrict := selected_actual_fixed_functional_rhs_lt_matching_mass
    (rho := ActualSelectedSpectralParameters.actualSelectedRho m h)
    (n := n) (c := c) (s := s) (h := h) (m := m) (J := J)
    (E := ActualStarFixedRhoDimensionGuard.badExponent m h)
    (e := e) Cc Tc fCoord hm256 hLowFloor rfl rfl
    (by simpa [c, s, h] using hsplit) hdiv rfl hrd he0 he'
    hfail (by simpa [c, h] using hcReal) (by simpa [s, h] using hsReal)
    hrhoFixed hdim hmassdim (by simpa only [mul_assoc] using hselectorNorm)
    haddBetaNorm hHC hJ (by rfl)
  have hmassEqReal :
      (ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.matchingStarMass
        (V := ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) (m := m)
        (Nat.le_add_right c s)
        (hcoordDim) Cc Tc fCoord : Real) =
      (ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.matchingStarMass
        (V := A.1) (m := m) (Nat.le_add_right c s)
        (hsourceDim)
        (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedCenterTable I copies U A C)
        (ActualTaggedComplementStarDensityBridge.transportedLeafTable I copies U A T)
        fSource : Real) := by
    have heq := hmassEq
    simpa [Cc, Tc, fc, hscoreEq] using congrArg (fun x : Rat => (x : Real)) heq.symm
  have hbound :
      (ActualOrdinaryStarWeightedSelection.matchingStarMass
        (V := ActualComplementCoordinateMassBridge.CoordAmbient J) (m := m)
        (Nat.le_add_right c s)
        (hcoordDim) Cc Tc fCoord : Real) <=
